# Rudolph et al. shallow MPS circuit decomposition

This project independently reproduces the paper's main protocol
`Iter[D_i O_all]`. It intentionally does not implement all five ablation
baselines or early stopping. Native compilation and FakeWashingtonV2 noise are
available only as a read-only downstream evaluation of a frozen paper-method
artifact; they do not alter the reproduced algorithm.

The implementation is convention-explicit and audit-oriented:

- `complex128`, q0 most significant;
- each macro-layer has exactly `N-1` ordered adjacent U(4) gates;
- Ran's terminal U(2) is folded into the last U(4);
- every new analytic layer is prepended to physical execution;
- all existing gates receive fixed-budget forward sweeps;
- the residual is recomputed from the optimized circuit before the next layer;
- paper infidelity is `1 - abs(<target|prepared>)`;
- squared fidelity is also exported for cross-paper comparison.

## Install

```bash
conda create -n rudolph2023-py311 python=3.11 -y
conda activate rudolph2023-py311
python -m pip install --upgrade pip
python -m pip install -e '.[dev,plot]'
```

For the optional calibrated-noise evaluation:

```bash
python -m pip install -e '.[dev,plot,noise]'
```

## Validation

```bash
python -m pytest -q
python -m pytest -q -m 'not slow'
python -m ruff check .
python -m compileall -q src tests scripts
```

The complete suite includes three `N=12, K=8, T=10` paper-scale regression
cases. The session audit fails unless every source-defined function/method is
called by the tests.

## Smoke

```bash
python -m rudolph2023 doctor
python -m rudolph2023 smoke --output artifacts/ubuntu_smoke
python -m rudolph2023 verify-artifact artifacts/ubuntu_smoke
```

## One many-body target

```bash
python -m rudolph2023 run \
  --target tfim \
  --num-qubits 12 \
  --coupling 1.0 \
  --field-x 1.0 \
  --depth 8 \
  --sweeps 10 \
  --output artifacts/tfim_n12_k8_t10
```

## FakeWashingtonV2 native-noise post-processing

First produce and verify a noiseless artifact as above. Then run:

```bash
python -m rudolph2023 native-noise \
  --artifact artifacts/tfim_n12_k8_t10 \
  --output artifacts/tfim_n12_k8_t10_washington \
  --optimization-level 0 \
  --seed-transpiler 2026 \
  --seed-simulator 2026 \
  --max-density-matrix-qubits 12
```

Omitting the physical path selects a deterministic connected path. Read that
path from the output manifest, then pass it explicitly for formal cross-method
comparisons and require the reported backend snapshot SHA-256 to match.

The command exactly synthesizes each U(4) to `rz/sx/x/cx`, forbids routing and
SWAP insertion, and then simulates gate plus thermal-relaxation noise with
Aer's density-matrix method. It exports logical, compilation, noisy-target and
native-retention fidelities separately, plus noisy energy error per site when
the source artifact contains a Hamiltonian. No measurement/readout noise is
used, and the raw density matrix is not exported.

## Paper main-method grid

```bash
python scripts/run_paper_main_method.py \
  --output-root artifacts/figure4_main \
  --depth 8 \
  --sweeps 10 100 1000 \
  --random-seed 2023
```

See `docs/` for the derivation, convention ledger, function-level validation
matrix, known paper ambiguities, FakeWashingtonV2 protocol, and Ubuntu runbook.

# Rudolph 2023 主方法复现计划

本项目只复现论文的核心协议 `Iter[D_i O_all]`，目标是得到可审计、可重放的
MPS 到浅层双比特量子线路编码实现。

实现范围：

1. 从精确 residual statevector 建立 OBC MPS；
2. 对副本执行从左至右、最大键维数 χ=2 的 TT-SVD 截断；
3. 将 Ran-canonical χ≤2 MPS 精确映射为 staircase 层；
4. 把末端 U(2) 左乘折叠进最后一个 U(4)，保持每层恰好 N−1 个 U(4)；
5. 在线路输入端加入新解析层；
6. 对当前全部层执行固定 T 次 forward Gauss–Seidel sweep；
7. 每个门按论文式 (5) 构造环境、取 polar 最优门，并按式 (6)、r=0.6 更新；
8. 从优化后的完整线路重新计算 residual，再进入下一深度；
9. 导出有序 U(4)、目标态、制备态、论文幅度 infidelity、平方保真度、哈希和运行来源；
10. 对基态任务额外导出 Hamiltonian、制备态能量和每格点能量误差。

当前不复现另外五种消融协议或 Figure 5 的 50 次统计。原生门编译和
FakeWashingtonV2 噪声作为冻结论文线路的独立只读后处理提供，不属于论文方法，
也不会改变 `Iter[D_i O_all]` 的理想基线结果。

验证分为四层：小系统独立解析 oracle、随机性质测试、与 Ran 2020/Ben-Dov 2024
独立实现的交叉验证，以及 N=12、K=8、T=10 的论文尺度回归。函数入口审计只用于
证明没有未执行源码函数，不单独作为数学正确性的证据。



========================================================================
文件路径: docs/CONVENTION_LEDGER_CN.md
========================================================================
# 约定台账

以下约定会直接影响门矩阵、线路顺序和跨论文数值比较。

| 项目 | 本复现约定 |
|---|---|
| 数值类型 | state/MPS/gate 均为 `complex128`，实数诊断为 `float64` |
| 比特序 | q0 为最高有效位；statevector 索引按 `q0...qN-1` 二进制解释 |
| U(4) 局域基 | `00, 01, 10, 11`；矩阵行为输出、列为输入 |
| MPS 轴序 | `(chi_left, physical, chi_right)` |
| 单层定义 | 相邻门 `(0,1),(1,2),...,(N-2,N-1)` 依次物理执行 |
| 线路层序 | `layers[0]` 最先物理执行；新的解析层 prepend 到输入端 |
| 末端 U(2) | `(I2 ⊗ U2) @ U4_last`，不另计单比特门 |
| 目标全局相位 | 最大模振幅中最小索引者固定为正实数 |
| 论文 fidelity | `f = abs(<target|prepared>)` |
| 论文 infidelity | `1 - f`，不是 `1 - f**2` |
| 跨论文 fidelity | 同时导出 `abs(<.|.>)**2` |
| Heisenberg | `S=σ/2`，3×4 row-major OBC，共 17 条边 |
| BAS | 2×6 row-major，66 个唯一图样的等幅叠加 |
| random MPS | OBC 可行键维数上限 64，张量实部独立采样 N(0,1) |
| polar 目标 | 最大化 `Re Tr(F†U)`；数值零空间采用确定性补全 |
| 式 (6) | `U <- U @ (U† @ U_opt)**r`，默认 `r=0.6` |
| 分数幂 | complex-Schur 严格主分支；仅精确负实轴点取 `+π` |
| sweep | 仅 forward，旧后缀和已更新前缀组成 Gauss–Seidel 更新 |

论文没有公布 Figure 4 使用的 random-MPS seed，也没有规定秩亏酉补全、简并 SVD
子空间或分数幂切口的全部数值规范。因此本项目把这些选择写入 manifest，而不把某个
随机样本声称为论文原始样本的逐位复现。

# 验证协议与通过标准

## 快速门槛

```bash
python -m compileall -q src tests scripts
python -m ruff check src tests scripts
python -m pytest -q -m 'not slow'
```

## 完整门槛

```bash
python -m pytest -q
```

完整门槛包含 N=12、K=8、T=10 的 Heisenberg 3×4、BAS 2×6 和 random-MPS
seed=2023 回归。必须同时满足：

- 测试零失败；
- `artifacts/function_call_audit.json` 的 `status=passed`、`missing=[]`；
- 每组线路形状 `(8,11,4,4)`；
- 每组总更新数 3960；
- 环境与 residual 恒等式误差小于 `1e-10`；
- Heisenberg 基态能量为 `-6.691680193514956 ± 2e-10`；
- 固定环境下 T=10 的最终幅度保真度与本项目回归值在 `2e-3` 内。

回归值不是论文原始数据文件，也不能取代公式级验证。它用于发现代码、BLAS 或分支约定
变化；允许 `2e-3` 是因为简并 SVD 子空间和秩亏补全的门 gauge 可跨平台不同。

## 跨实现验证

```bash
python scripts/cross_validate_prior_implementations.py \
  --ran-root /path/to/Ran_2020 \
  --bendov-root /path/to/Ben_Dov_2024 \
  --output artifacts/cross_project_validation.json
```

报告 `status` 必须为 `passed`，所有最大误差必须小于 `1e-11`。

## 产物验证含义

`verify-artifact` 会验证当前源码树、内部文件/数组哈希、有限性、归一化、线路结构、门重放、
全部最终 fidelity、更新数恒等式以及可选 Hamiltonian 能量。`SHA256SUMS.json` 没有外部
签名，因此它能发现意外损坏和内部不一致，不能抵御攻击者同时篡改全部文件；文档不把它
描述成密码学签名。

# Ubuntu 验证与正式运行手册

假设代码位于 `/home/cg/PycharmProjects/Paper_Reproductions/Rudolph_2023`。

## 1. 创建环境

```bash
cd /home/cg/PycharmProjects/Paper_Reproductions/Rudolph_2023

conda create -n rudolph2023-py311 python=3.11 -y
conda activate rudolph2023-py311

python -m pip install --upgrade pip
python -m pip install -e '.[dev,plot,noise]'

export OMP_NUM_THREADS=1
export MKL_NUM_THREADS=1
export OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS=1
export NUMEXPR_NUM_THREADS=1
```

## 2. 完整工程验证

```bash
python --version
python -m rudolph2023 doctor | tee artifacts/ubuntu_doctor.json
python -m compileall -q src tests scripts
python -m ruff check src tests scripts
python -m pytest -q

python - <<'PY'
import json
from pathlib import Path

audit = json.loads(Path('artifacts/function_call_audit.json').read_text())
assert audit['status'] == 'passed'
assert audit['missing'] == []
print('function audit:', audit['called_count'], '/', audit['expected_count'])
print('source tree:', audit['source_tree_sha256'])
print('test tree:', audit['test_tree_sha256'])
PY
```

## 3. 最小端到端重放

```bash
python -m rudolph2023 smoke --output artifacts/ubuntu_smoke
python -m rudolph2023 verify-artifact artifacts/ubuntu_smoke
```

## 4. 多体基态编码示例

```bash
python -m rudolph2023 run \
  --target tfim \
  --num-qubits 12 \
  --coupling 1.0 \
  --field-x 1.0 \
  --depth 8 \
  --sweeps 10 \
  --output artifacts/tfim_n12_k8_t10

python -m rudolph2023 verify-artifact artifacts/tfim_n12_k8_t10
```

输出同时包含 `paper_infidelity`、`squared_state_fidelity`、`prepared_energy` 和
`energy_error_per_site`。

## 5. Figure 4 主方法长跑

先做 T=10：

```bash
python scripts/run_paper_main_method.py \
  --output-root artifacts/figure4_iter_di_oall_t10 \
  --depth 8 \
  --sweeps 10 \
  --random-seed 2023
```

确认三个 artifact 均 verified 后，再启动 T=100/1000：

```bash
python scripts/run_paper_main_method.py \
  --output-root artifacts/figure4_iter_di_oall_t100_t1000 \
  --depth 8 \
  --sweeps 100 1000 \
  --random-seed 2023
```

建议在 `tmux` 中运行，并保留终端日志。T=1000 的总门更新量是 T=10 的 100 倍，运行
时间也近似线性增加。

## 6. FakeWashingtonV2 噪声验证

先对一个小系统执行真实校准后端 smoke，再运行 N=12。详细边界和指标定义见
`FAKE_WASHINGTON_NOISE_CN.md`。

```bash
python -m rudolph2023 run \
  --target tfim \
  --num-qubits 2 \
  --field-x 0.7 \
  --depth 2 \
  --sweeps 2 \
  --output artifacts/tfim_n2_noise_source

python -m rudolph2023 native-noise \
  --artifact artifacts/tfim_n2_noise_source \
  --output artifacts/tfim_n2_fake_washington \
  --physical-path 0 1 \
  --max-density-matrix-qubits 2

(cd artifacts/tfim_n2_fake_washington && sha256sum --check SHA256SUMS)
```

正式跨方法比较必须显式冻结同一 `physical_path`，并核对
`backend_snapshot_sha256`。N=12 密度矩阵裸数据约 256 MiB，建议串行运行。

## 7. 完整性检查

```bash
find artifacts -type f -print0 | sort -z | xargs -0 sha256sum \
  > artifacts/ubuntu_all_files_sha256.txt
sha256sum artifacts/ubuntu_all_files_sha256.txt
```

正式结果回传时至少保留每个 run 的 `manifest.json`、`result_arrays.npz`、
`SHA256SUMS.json`，以及总汇总 JSON。

========================================================================
文件路径: docs/METHOD_DERIVATION_CN.md
========================================================================
# `Iter[D_i O_all]` 实现推导

设目标态为 `|ψ>`，当前制备线路为 `C_k`，其 residual 定义为

\[
|r_k\rangle=C_k^\dagger|\psi\rangle.
\]

## 解析增长 `D_i`

对 `|r_k>` 的 MPS 副本做 χ=2 TT-SVD，得到 `|r_k^(2)>`。原 residual 本身不被
截断。Ran-canonical χ≤2 MPS 的每个张量给出一个行等距映射，把规定列嵌入 U(4)
并确定性补全。末端 U(2) 按

\[
U_{N-2}'=(I_2\otimes U_2)U_{N-2}
\]

折叠，所以解析层 `L_{k+1}` 只有 N−1 个 U(4)。它满足

\[
L_{k+1}|0^N\rangle=|r_k^{(2)}\rangle,
\quad
\langle 0|L_{k+1}^\dagger|r_k\rangle
=\langle r_k^{(2)}|r_k\rangle.
\]

候选线路应为

\[
C_{k+1}^{(0)}=C_kL_{k+1}.
\]

由于矩阵右端先作用，新层必须放在物理执行序列最前端。代码中的 `prepend_input_layer`
正是这一含义。

## 全线路优化 `O_all`

把当前线路展平为物理执行门 `U_1,...,U_M`。更新第 m 个门时，缓存

\[
|p_m\rangle=U'_{m-1}\cdots U'_1|0\rangle,
\quad
|s_m\rangle=U_{m+1}^\dagger\cdots U_M^\dagger|\psi\rangle.
\]

环境矩阵 `F_m` 被构造成

\[
\operatorname{Tr}(F_m^\dagger U_m)
=\langle s_m|U_m|p_m\rangle.
\]

若 `F_m=WΣV†`，则 polar 最优门为 `U_opt=WV†`。论文式 (6) 的阻尼更新为

\[
U_m' = U_m\left(U_m^\dagger U_{opt}\right)^r,
\qquad r=0.6.
\]

一次 sweep 按 m 从小到大执行，因此前缀使用本 sweep 已更新门，后缀使用 sweep 开始时
的未来门。这是 forward Gauss–Seidel，而不是 Jacobi。分数主幂在切口附近不保证
`r=0.6` 的每个局域步骤单调，因此实现忠实记录负增益，不偷偷改成线搜索。

在深度 k 完成 T 次 sweep 后，用优化后的整个 `C_k` 重新计算 `C_k†|ψ>`。最终 K
深度累计门更新数必须为

\[
(N-1)T\sum_{k=1}^{K}k
=(N-1)T K(K+1)/2.
\]

例如 N=12、K=8、T=10 时恰为 3960 次。

# 当前复现状态

状态：`Iter[D_i O_all]` 主方法已实现，工程审计通过。

已完成：

- 纯 U(4) staircase 解析层；
- χ=2 TT-SVD 与未截断 residual 的正确分离；
- 全线路 forward polar 优化与 r=0.6 式 (6)；
- N=12 三类论文目标；
- TFIM、XXZ 等附加多体基态目标；
- 幅度/平方保真度和基态能量指标；
- 自包含 Hamiltonian CSR 产物、ordered U(4)、statevector 与 SHA-256；
- 逐函数入口审计、独立 oracle、跨实现和论文尺度回归。

审计中特别修复：Schur 负实轴附近错误吸附、非默认 `rank_rtol` 破坏 polar 最优性、
MPS 极端值溢出被误归一化、BAS CLI 默认误成 3×4、基态能量未导出，以及只改 manifest
并重算 checksum 仍能假通过的问题。

边界说明：

- 论文未公开 Figure 4 random-MPS seed，本项目默认 seed=2023，仅作为可复现实例；
- 精确简并基态没有唯一目标向量，本项目要求显式 symmetry/tie-break，否则拒绝；
- T=100/1000 的正式长跑应在 Ubuntu 主机完成；
- 论文方法复现范围仍不含另外五种协议；现已另设只读后处理层，将冻结的
  `Iter[D_i O_all]` U(4) 线路精确综合到 FakeWashingtonV2 原生门并执行 Aer
  密度矩阵噪声评估。该扩展不改变或重新训练论文方法。

文件路径: docs/FUNCTION_VALIDATION_MATRIX_CN.md

# 逐函数验证矩阵

“调用过”与“正确”是两回事。本项目对每个函数至少要求入口被完整测试套件执行；核心
科学函数还必须具有不共享实现路径的 oracle 或交叉实现证据。

| 模块 | 功能 | 主要独立证据 |
|---|---|---|
| `conventions` | dtype、归一化、相位、比特数 | 错误输入、相位等价、范数边界测试 |
| `linalg` | SVD、rank、polar、Schur 分数幂、式 (6) | 重构；nuclear-norm oracle；切口两侧；端点 r=0/1；非默认 rank 阈值 |
| `mps` | dense/MPS、canonical、χ 截断 | dense round-trip；NumPy vdot；Eckart–Young；未归一输入；溢出/下溢负测 |
| `completion` | 等距列到酉矩阵 | 规定列逐位保持、unitarity、空列补全、整数类型负测 |
| `circuit` | U(4) 执行、层/线路顺序 | 独立 full-Kronecker matrix oracle、任意输入 U(2) 折叠、空线路宽度负测 |
| `analytic` | χ=2 层和 `D_i` | Ran_2020 独立实现；一般随机 row-isometry；全部 N≤6 基态；GHZ/W |
| `optimizer` | 环境、suffix、polar sweep | 16 个局域矩阵元逐一替换并全线路重放；Ben-Dov 独立实现；naive sweep |
| `protocol` | 逐层增长、全局优化、residual | 相邻深度完整向量恒等式；全部旧层顺序；full-matrix 多层 oracle；更新数公式 |
| `targets` | Heisenberg/BAS/random/TFIM/XXZ | 小系统 Kronecker；singlet −3/4；dense/sparse ED；BAS 独立逻辑谓词；RNG 直比 |
| `metrics` | 两种 fidelity、能量、熵 | 手算态、norm 漂移、Bell 熵、非有限 Hamiltonian 负测 |
| `export` | U(4)/state/Hamiltonian/哈希/重放 | 文件和语义哈希；重算全部 final fidelity；重算能量；篡改后重哈希仍拒绝 |
| `cli` | doctor/smoke/五类 target | 每分支端到端；BAS 无参数默认 2×6；基态输出 ΔE/N |
| `qiskit_adapter` | q0-MSB 映射、U(4) 逻辑线路与精确原生综合 | 非对称随机 U(4) 的 full-Kronecker/Qiskit Operator/NumPy 三方 oracle；4 比特 3 层多 seed；累积全局相位；barrier 等价 |
| `washington_noise` | compact FakeWashington Target、无路由编译、密度矩阵噪声 | 校准逐字段复制；snapshot 确定性；无 SWAP/opaque unitary；零噪声能量；三点解析 depolarizing；trace/Hermiticity/PSD；真实 FakeWashingtonV2 smoke |
| `native_noise_protocol` | 冻结 artifact 的只读噪声后处理和可审计导出 | 原 U(4) 重放；两级编译 fidelity fail-closed；旧 artifact core-source 兼容校验；原子化 QPY/manifest/backend snapshot/SHA-256 导出；CLI 端到端 |

完整测试结束时，`artifacts/function_call_audit.json` 记录源码树与测试树 SHA-256，且
`missing=[]`。该文件只证明函数入口覆盖；数学正确性依据上表各类 oracle。

跨项目验证固定使用：20 个 N=6 复随机态对比 Ran_2020 的压缩态、制备态和 residual；
50 个环境对比 Ben-Dov_2024 的环境和 polar。容许误差阈值为 `1e-11`。



========================================================================
文件路径: docs/FAKE_WASHINGTON_NOISE_CN.md
========================================================================
# FakeWashingtonV2 原生编译与噪声评估

## 定位

该功能是论文复现结果的只读后处理层，不属于 Rudolph 等人在论文中提出的
`Iter[D_i O_all]` 优化本身。输入必须是已经由 `rudolph2023 run` 导出并通过完整性
校验的 artifact。噪声命令不会重新优化 U(4)、剪枝、近似综合、改变目标态或执行测量。

处理链为：

1. 从 `result_arrays.npz` 读取物理执行顺序的 U(4)；
2. 按 `project site i -> Qiskit qubit N-1-i` 映射线路；
3. 将每个 U(4) 独立且精确地综合为 `rz/sx/x/cx`；
4. 从 FakeWashingtonV2 复制一条连续物理路径的校准到 N 比特 compact Target；
5. 使用 identity initial layout、`routing_method=none` 和 `approximation_degree=1` 编译；
6. 拒绝任何 SWAP、残留 opaque `unitary`、非 identity final layout 或理想编译保真度失败；
7. 用 Aer density-matrix 方法施加门错误与热弛豫噪声。

不能直接模拟 127 比特 FakeWashingtonV2 的完整密度矩阵。本实现只模拟重编号后的
N 比特 compact backend，同时保存原物理比特路径和逐门校准。

## 指标

- 论文幅度保真度：`|<psi_target|psi_logical>|`；
- 逻辑全局平方保真度：`|<psi_target|psi_logical>|^2`；
- 编译保真度：`|<psi_logical|psi_native>|^2`；
- 噪声目标保真度：`<psi_target|rho_noisy|psi_target>`；
- 噪声线路保持率：`<psi_native|rho_noisy|psi_native>`；
- 基态能量误差/site：`(Tr[H rho_noisy]-E0)/N`。

所有 fidelity 均不做 clipping。论文的幅度 infidelity 与噪声混态平方保真度是不同
定义，导出文件不会把二者混写。

## 噪声边界

- 默认 `gate_error=true`、`thermal_relaxation=true`；
- 因为没有测量，`readout_error=false`；
- `shots=1` 只用于执行保存密度矩阵的无测量线路，保存的是 density-matrix 通道输出，
  不是有限 shots tomography；
- 模型不包含串扰、泄漏、校准漂移；
- 未插入 delay/scheduling，因此不声称完整模拟所有 idle 时段。

## Ubuntu 安装

```bash
conda create -n rudolph2023-py311 python=3.11 -y
conda activate rudolph2023-py311
python -m pip install --upgrade pip
python -m pip install -e '.[dev,plot,noise]'

python - <<'PY'
import qiskit
import qiskit_aer
import qiskit_ibm_runtime
print("qiskit:", qiskit.__version__)
print("qiskit-aer:", qiskit_aer.__version__)
print("qiskit-ibm-runtime:", qiskit_ibm_runtime.__version__)
PY
```

支持并审计的依赖范围是 Qiskit 2.2–2.x、Aer 0.17.x、IBM Runtime
0.45–0.48。正式结果应保留实际版本。

## 小规模真实后端验证

先生成 N=2 TFIM artifact：

```bash
python -m rudolph2023 run \
  --target tfim \
  --num-qubits 2 \
  --coupling 1.0 \
  --field-x 0.7 \
  --depth 2 \
  --sweeps 2 \
  --output artifacts/tfim_n2_noise_source

python -m rudolph2023 native-noise \
  --artifact artifacts/tfim_n2_noise_source \
  --output artifacts/tfim_n2_fake_washington \
  --physical-path 0 1 \
  --optimization-level 0 \
  --seed-transpiler 2026 \
  --seed-simulator 2026 \
  --max-density-matrix-qubits 2

cd artifacts/tfim_n2_fake_washington
sha256sum --check SHA256SUMS
```

应检查：

- `compilation_fidelity` 接近 1；
- `swap_count=0`、`unitary_count=0`；
- trace error 与 Hermiticity residual 接近机器精度；
- noisy target fidelity 是有限的 [0,1] 内数值；
- 后端 snapshot SHA-256、路径、QPY 和输入 artifact hashes 均存在。

## N=12 正式运行

N=12 的一个 complex128 密度矩阵裸数据约 256 MiB；Aer 内部对象、噪声通道和诊断会
需要更多内存。应逐个 artifact 串行运行，不并行保存多个密度矩阵。

首次可省略 `--physical-path`，让程序确定性选取一条连续路径：

```bash
python -m rudolph2023 native-noise \
  --artifact artifacts/tfim_n12_k8_t10 \
  --output artifacts/tfim_n12_k8_t10_washington_auto \
  --optimization-level 0 \
  --seed-transpiler 2026 \
  --seed-simulator 2026 \
  --max-density-matrix-qubits 12
```

从输出的 `native_noise_manifest.json` 读取 `noise_protocol.physical_path`。正式跨方法比较
时，把这条路径显式写回所有方法的命令，并要求
`backend_snapshot_sha256` 完全一致。自动路径是确定性的 smoke 约定，不代表错误率最优路径。

## 输出

- `native_noise_manifest.json`：输入来源、编译诊断、全部指标和环境；
- `fake_washington_compact_snapshot.json`：路径、端序映射和校准；
- `native_circuit.qpy`：可重放原生线路；
- `SHA256SUMS`：上述文件的 SHA-256。

默认不导出原始密度矩阵，以避免 N=12 产生大文件并降低误用风险。

## 结论

Rudolph 线路到 FakeWashingtonV2 噪声密度矩阵的完整链路已实现，并通过组件 oracle、
真实 fake backend 小系统端到端测试和全项目逐函数入口审计。该功能是冻结论文结果的
只读后处理，不改变 `Iter[D_i O_all]`。

"""Paper-native and cross-paper state/energy metrics."""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any

import numpy as np
from numpy.typing import ArrayLike

from .conventions import infer_num_qubits, validate_normalized_statevector


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class FidelityMetrics:
    """All fidelity conventions needed to avoid cross-paper ambiguity."""

    overlap: complex
    amplitude_fidelity: float
    paper_amplitude_infidelity: float
    squared_state_fidelity: float
    squared_infidelity: float


def fidelity_metrics(target: ArrayLike, candidate: ArrayLike) -> FidelityMetrics:
    """Return amplitude and squared fidelities for two normalized pure states."""

    target_state = validate_normalized_statevector(target)
    candidate_state = validate_normalized_statevector(candidate)
    if target_state.shape != candidate_state.shape:
        raise ValueError("Target and candidate statevector shapes differ.")
    # The validator deliberately permits machine-scale norm drift.  Remove it
    # before taking the overlap so a pair of physically identical states can
    # never produce a fidelity above one or a negative infidelity.
    target_state = target_state / np.linalg.norm(target_state)
    candidate_state = candidate_state / np.linalg.norm(candidate_state)
    overlap = complex(np.vdot(target_state, candidate_state))
    amplitude = float(np.clip(abs(overlap), 0.0, 1.0))
    squared = float(np.clip(amplitude**2, 0.0, 1.0))
    return FidelityMetrics(
        overlap=overlap,
        amplitude_fidelity=amplitude,
        paper_amplitude_infidelity=1.0 - amplitude,
        squared_state_fidelity=squared,
        squared_infidelity=1.0 - squared,
    )


def energy_expectation(state: ArrayLike, hamiltonian: ArrayLike | Any) -> float:
    """Return a real normalized energy expectation for dense or sparse H."""

    vector = validate_normalized_statevector(state)
    dimension = vector.size
    if getattr(hamiltonian, "shape", None) != (dimension, dimension):
        raise ValueError("Hamiltonian shape does not match state dimension.")
    evolved = hamiltonian @ vector
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(evolved)):
        raise FloatingPointError("Hamiltonian action produced non-finite values.")
    value = complex(np.vdot(vector, evolved))
    if not math.isfinite(value.real) or not math.isfinite(value.imag):
        raise FloatingPointError("Energy expectation is non-finite.")
    scale = max(1.0, abs(value.real))
    if abs(value.imag) > 1e-11 * scale:
        raise FloatingPointError(f"Energy has a nonphysical imaginary part: {value.imag:.3e}.")
    return float(value.real)


def energy_error_per_site(
    state: ArrayLike,
    hamiltonian: ArrayLike | Any,
    ground_energy: float,
) -> float:
    """Return ``(E(state)-E0)/N`` with tiny negative roundoff clipped to zero."""

    if not isinstance(ground_energy, int | float | np.floating):
        raise TypeError("ground_energy must be real.")
    ground_energy = float(ground_energy)
    if not math.isfinite(ground_energy):
        raise ValueError("ground_energy must be finite.")
    num_qubits = infer_num_qubits(state)
    error = (energy_expectation(state, hamiltonian) - ground_energy) / num_qubits
    if error < 0.0 and error >= -1e-11:
        return 0.0
    if error < -1e-11:
        raise ArithmeticError(
            "Energy lies below the supplied ground energy; conventions or E0 are inconsistent."
        )
    return float(error)


def entanglement_entropy(state: ArrayLike, cut: int) -> float:
    """Return von Neumann entropy from squared Schmidt coefficients."""

    vector = validate_normalized_statevector(state)
    num_qubits = infer_num_qubits(vector)
    if isinstance(cut, bool) or not isinstance(cut, int | np.integer):
        raise TypeError("cut must be an integer.")
    cut = int(cut)
    if not 1 <= cut < num_qubits:
        raise ValueError("cut must lie strictly inside the chain.")
    singular_values = np.linalg.svd(
        vector.reshape(1 << cut, 1 << (num_qubits - cut)),
        compute_uv=False,
    )
    probabilities = np.square(singular_values)
    probabilities = probabilities[probabilities > 0.0]
    return float(-np.sum(probabilities * np.log(probabilities)))


__all__ = [
    "FidelityMetrics",
    "energy_error_per_site",
    "energy_expectation",
    "entanglement_entropy",
    "fidelity_metrics",
]

"""Open-boundary qubit MPS primitives and audited chi=2 TT-SVD truncation."""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
import numbers
from collections.abc import Iterable, Iterator, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass

import numpy as np
from numpy.typing import ArrayLike, NDArray

from .conventions import COMPLEX_DTYPE, as_complex128, infer_num_qubits
from .linalg import deterministic_svd, svd_numerical_rank

ComplexArray = NDArray[np.complex128]
RealArray = NDArray[np.float64]
MPSLike = "MPS | Sequence[ArrayLike]"


def _coerce_tensor(tensor: ArrayLike, *, copy: bool) -> ComplexArray:
    array = np.asarray(tensor)
    if array.ndim != 3:
        raise ValueError(f"MPS tensor must have three axes, got {array.shape}.")
    result = (
        np.array(array, dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE, copy=True)
        if copy
        else np.asarray(array, dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE)
    )
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(result)):
        raise ValueError("MPS tensors must contain only finite values.")
    return result


def validate_mps(tensors: MPSLike) -> tuple[ComplexArray, ...]:
    """Validate an OBC qubit MPS in ``(chi_left, physical, chi_right)`` order."""

    raw = tensors.tensors if isinstance(tensors, MPS) else tuple(tensors)
    if not raw:
        raise ValueError("An MPS must contain at least one tensor.")
    checked = tuple(_coerce_tensor(tensor, copy=False) for tensor in raw)
    if checked[0].shape[0] != 1 or checked[-1].shape[2] != 1:
        raise ValueError("Open-boundary MPS endpoint bond dimensions must be one.")
    for site, tensor in enumerate(checked):
        if tensor.shape[1] != 2:
            raise ValueError(f"Site {site} must have physical dimension two.")
        if tensor.shape[0] < 1 or tensor.shape[2] < 1:
            raise ValueError(f"Site {site} has a nonpositive bond dimension.")
        if site and checked[site - 1].shape[2] != tensor.shape[0]:
            raise ValueError(f"Bond mismatch between sites {site - 1} and {site}.")
    return checked


@dataclass(frozen=True, init=False)
class MPS(Sequence[ComplexArray]):
    """Owned complex128 open-boundary qubit MPS container."""

    tensors: tuple[ComplexArray, ...]

    def __init__(self, tensors: Iterable[ArrayLike]) -> None:
        owned = tuple(_coerce_tensor(tensor, copy=True) for tensor in tensors)
        validate_mps(owned)
        object.__setattr__(self, "tensors", owned)

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        return len(self.tensors)

    def __iter__(self) -> Iterator[ComplexArray]:
        return iter(self.tensors)

    def __getitem__(self, item: int | slice) -> ComplexArray | tuple[ComplexArray, ...]:
        return self.tensors[item]

    @property
    def num_sites(self) -> int:
        return len(self.tensors)

    @property
    def bond_dimensions(self) -> tuple[int, ...]:
        return tuple(int(tensor.shape[2]) for tensor in self.tensors[:-1])

    @property
    def max_bond_dimension(self) -> int:
        return max(self.bond_dimensions, default=1)

    def copy(self) -> MPS:
        return type(self)(tensor.copy() for tensor in self.tensors)

    def to_dense(self) -> ComplexArray:
        return mps_to_dense(self)

    def norm(self) -> float:
        return mps_norm(self)


def as_mps(mps: MPSLike, *, copy: bool = False) -> MPS:
    """Return a validated MPS, with an optional deep copy."""

    if isinstance(mps, MPS):
        return mps.copy() if copy else mps
    return MPS(mps)


def mps_to_dense(mps: MPSLike) -> ComplexArray:
    """Contract an MPS into a q0-most-significant statevector."""

    tensors = validate_mps(mps)
    state = tensors[0][0, :, :]
    for tensor in tensors[1:]:
        state = np.tensordot(state, tensor, axes=([-1], [0]))
    return np.asarray(state[..., 0], dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE).reshape(-1)


def dense_to_mps(
    state: ArrayLike,
    *,
    max_bond: int | None = None,
    normalize: bool = False,
) -> MPS:
    """Factor a state right-to-left into the Ran row-isometric gauge."""

    vector = as_complex128(state, copy=True).reshape(-1)
    num_qubits = infer_num_qubits(vector)
    raw_norm = float(np.linalg.norm(vector))
    if raw_norm <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(raw_norm):
        raise ValueError("Statevector must have a finite nonzero norm.")
    if normalize:
        vector /= raw_norm
    if max_bond is not None:
        if isinstance(max_bond, bool) or not isinstance(max_bond, numbers.Integral):
            raise TypeError("max_bond must be an integer or None.")
        if max_bond < 1:
            raise ValueError("max_bond must be positive.")

    remaining = vector.reshape((2,) * num_qubits)
    right_bond = 1
    reversed_tensors: list[ComplexArray] = []
    for _site in range(num_qubits - 1, 0, -1):
        matrix = remaining.reshape(-1, 2 * right_bond)
        u_matrix, singular_values, vh_matrix = deterministic_svd(matrix, pivot_on="vh")
        rank = svd_numerical_rank(singular_values, matrix.shape)
        if max_bond is not None:
            rank = min(rank, int(max_bond))
        reversed_tensors.append(vh_matrix[:rank].reshape(rank, 2, right_bond))
        remaining = u_matrix[:, :rank] * singular_values[:rank]
        right_bond = rank
    first = remaining.reshape(1, 2, right_bond)
    result = MPS([first, *reversed(reversed_tensors)])
    return normalize_mps(result) if normalize else result


def mps_inner_product(bra: MPSLike, ket: MPSLike) -> complex:
    """Contract ``<bra|ket>`` without changing either norm."""

    bra_tensors = validate_mps(bra)
    ket_tensors = validate_mps(ket)
    if len(bra_tensors) != len(ket_tensors):
        raise ValueError("MPS site counts differ.")
    environment = np.ones((1, 1), dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE)
    for bra_tensor, ket_tensor in zip(bra_tensors, ket_tensors, strict=True):
        with np.errstate(over="ignore", invalid="ignore", under="ignore"):
            environment = np.einsum(
                "ab,api,bpj->ij",
                environment,
                bra_tensor.conj(),
                ket_tensor,
                optimize=True,
            )
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(environment)):
            raise FloatingPointError("MPS contraction overflowed or became non-finite.")
    value = complex(environment[0, 0])
    if not math.isfinite(value.real) or not math.isfinite(value.imag):
        raise FloatingPointError("MPS inner product is non-finite.")
    return value


def mps_norm(mps: MPSLike) -> float:
    """Return the MPS norm after checking numerical reality and positivity."""

    value = mps_inner_product(mps, mps)
    if not math.isfinite(value.real) or not math.isfinite(value.imag):
        raise FloatingPointError("MPS squared norm is non-finite.")
    scale = max(1.0, abs(value.real))
    if abs(value.imag) > 1e-12 * scale or value.real < -1e-13 * scale:
        raise FloatingPointError(f"Invalid MPS squared norm: {value}.")
    norm = math.sqrt(max(0.0, float(value.real)))
    if not math.isfinite(norm):
        raise FloatingPointError("MPS norm is non-finite.")
    return norm


def normalize_mps(mps: MPSLike) -> MPS:
    """Normalize an MPS by scaling only its first tensor."""

    state = as_mps(mps, copy=True)
    norm = mps_norm(state)
    if not math.isfinite(norm):
        raise FloatingPointError("Cannot normalize an MPS with non-finite norm.")
    if norm <= 0.0:
        raise ValueError("Cannot normalize a zero MPS.")
    tensors = list(state.tensors)
    tensors[0] = tensors[0] / norm
    return MPS(tensors)


def canonicalize_to_ran(mps: MPSLike, *, normalize: bool = True) -> MPS:
    """Sweep right-to-left so each tensor obeys ``A A^dagger=I``."""

    state = as_mps(mps, copy=True)
    raw_norm = mps_norm(state)
    if not math.isfinite(raw_norm):
        raise FloatingPointError("Cannot canonicalize an MPS with non-finite norm.")
    if raw_norm <= 0.0:
        raise ValueError("Cannot canonicalize a zero MPS.")
    tensors = [tensor.copy() for tensor in state.tensors]
    for site in range(state.num_sites - 1, 0, -1):
        tensor = tensors[site]
        matrix = tensor.reshape(tensor.shape[0], tensor.shape[1] * tensor.shape[2])
        u_matrix, singular_values, vh_matrix = deterministic_svd(matrix, pivot_on="vh")
        rank = svd_numerical_rank(singular_values, matrix.shape)
        tensors[site] = vh_matrix[:rank].reshape(rank, tensor.shape[1], tensor.shape[2])
        transfer = u_matrix[:, :rank] * singular_values[:rank]
        tensors[site - 1] = np.einsum(
            "lpm,mr->lpr", tensors[site - 1], transfer, optimize=True
        )
    if normalize:
        tensors[0] /= raw_norm
    return MPS(tensors)


def ran_isometry_residuals(mps: MPSLike) -> tuple[float, ...]:
    """Return per-site normalized Frobenius residuals of ``A A^dagger=I``."""

    residuals: list[float] = []
    for tensor in validate_mps(mps):
        matrix = tensor.reshape(tensor.shape[0], -1)
        identity = np.eye(matrix.shape[0], dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE)
        error = matrix @ matrix.conj().T - identity
        residuals.append(float(np.linalg.norm(error) / math.sqrt(identity.shape[0])))
    return tuple(residuals)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class TruncationResult:
    """A chi-limited MPS and complete TT-SVD diagnostics."""

    mps: MPS
    max_bond: int
    kept_ranks: tuple[int, ...]
    singular_values: tuple[RealArray, ...]
    discarded_weights: tuple[float, ...]
    input_norm: float
    truncated_norm_before_normalization: float
    output_norm: float
    normalized_overlap: float
    squared_fidelity: float
    isometry_residuals: tuple[float, ...]

    @property
    def total_discarded_weight(self) -> float:
        return float(sum(self.discarded_weights))


def truncate_mps(
    mps: MPSLike,
    *,
    max_bond: int,
    normalize: bool = True,
) -> TruncationResult:
    """Perform deterministic canonical TT-SVD rounding to ``max_bond``."""

    if isinstance(max_bond, bool) or not isinstance(max_bond, numbers.Integral):
        raise TypeError("max_bond must be an integer.")
    if max_bond < 1:
        raise ValueError("max_bond must be positive.")
    original = as_mps(mps)
    input_norm = mps_norm(original)
    if not math.isfinite(input_norm):
        raise FloatingPointError("Cannot truncate an MPS with non-finite norm.")
    right_canonical = canonicalize_to_ran(original, normalize=False)
    tensors = [tensor.copy() for tensor in right_canonical.tensors]
    singular_values_by_bond: list[RealArray] = []
    discarded_weights: list[float] = []
    kept_ranks: list[int] = []
    for site in range(original.num_sites - 1):
        tensor = tensors[site]
        matrix = tensor.reshape(tensor.shape[0] * 2, tensor.shape[2])
        u_matrix, singular_values, vh_matrix = deterministic_svd(matrix, pivot_on="u")
        rank = svd_numerical_rank(singular_values, matrix.shape)
        keep = min(int(max_bond), rank)
        singular_values_by_bond.append(singular_values.copy())
        discarded_weights.append(float(np.sum(np.square(singular_values[keep:]))))
        kept_ranks.append(keep)
        tensors[site] = u_matrix[:, :keep].reshape(tensor.shape[0], 2, keep)
        transfer = singular_values[:keep, None] * vh_matrix[:keep]
        tensors[site + 1] = np.einsum(
            "km,mpn->kpn", transfer, tensors[site + 1], optimize=True
        )

    truncated = MPS(tensors)
    truncated_norm = mps_norm(truncated)
    if not math.isfinite(truncated_norm):
        raise FloatingPointError("TT-SVD approximation has a non-finite norm.")
    if truncated_norm <= 0.0:
        raise FloatingPointError("TT-SVD produced a zero approximation.")
    output = canonicalize_to_ran(truncated, normalize=normalize)
    output_norm = mps_norm(output)
    if not math.isfinite(output_norm) or output_norm <= 0.0:
        raise FloatingPointError("TT-SVD output has an invalid norm.")
    overlap = mps_inner_product(original, output) / (input_norm * output_norm)
    amplitude = float(abs(overlap))
    return TruncationResult(
        mps=output,
        max_bond=int(max_bond),
        kept_ranks=tuple(kept_ranks),
        singular_values=tuple(singular_values_by_bond),
        discarded_weights=tuple(discarded_weights),
        input_norm=input_norm,
        truncated_norm_before_normalization=truncated_norm,
        output_norm=output_norm,
        normalized_overlap=amplitude,
        squared_fidelity=amplitude**2,
        isometry_residuals=ran_isometry_residuals(output),
    )


def open_boundary_bond_dimensions(num_qubits: int, max_bond: int) -> tuple[int, ...]:
    """Return the maximal feasible OBC bond profile capped at ``max_bond``."""

    if isinstance(num_qubits, bool) or not isinstance(num_qubits, numbers.Integral):
        raise TypeError("num_qubits must be an integer.")
    if isinstance(max_bond, bool) or not isinstance(max_bond, numbers.Integral):
        raise TypeError("max_bond must be an integer.")
    if num_qubits < 2 or max_bond < 1:
        raise ValueError("num_qubits must be >=2 and max_bond positive.")
    return tuple(
        min(1 << (bond + 1), int(max_bond), 1 << (num_qubits - bond - 1))
        for bond in range(num_qubits - 1)
    )


__all__ = [
    "MPS",
    "TruncationResult",
    "as_mps",
    "canonicalize_to_ran",
    "dense_to_mps",
    "mps_inner_product",
    "mps_norm",
    "mps_to_dense",
    "normalize_mps",
    "open_boundary_bond_dimensions",
    "ran_isometry_residuals",
    "truncate_mps",
    "validate_mps",
]

"""Command-line entry points for smoke and formal main-method runs."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import platform
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import numpy as np
import scipy
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix

from .export import save_reproduction_artifact, verify_reproduction_artifact
from .metrics import energy_error_per_site, energy_expectation
from .native_noise_protocol import run_artifact_native_noise_protocol
from .protocol import run_iter_di_o_all
from .targets import (
    bars_and_stripes_state,
    exact_ground_state,
    heisenberg_grid_hamiltonian,
    random_real_mps,
    tfim_hamiltonian,
    xxz_hamiltonian,
)


def doctor_snapshot() -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Return a read-only numerical/convention snapshot."""

    return {
        "python": sys.version,
        "platform": platform.platform(),
        "numpy": np.__version__,
        "scipy": scipy.__version__,
        "protocol": "Iter[D_i O_all]",
        "learning_rate": 0.6,
        "complex_dtype": "complex128",
        "qubit_order": "q0-most-significant",
        "layer": "N-1 ordered adjacent U(4), no terminal U(2)",
        "fidelity": "paper infidelity = 1 - abs(<target|prepared>)",
        "fractional_power": "principal complex-Schur branch; -1 -> +pi",
        "target_global_phase": "first maximum-modulus amplitude positive real",
    }


def _build_target(
    args: argparse.Namespace,
) -> tuple[np.ndarray, str, dict[str, Any], csr_matrix | None]:
    if args.target == "random-mps":
        random = random_real_mps(
            args.num_qubits, max_bond=args.max_bond, seed=args.seed
        )
        return random.state, "random_real_mps", {
            "seed": args.seed,
            "max_bond": args.max_bond,
            "bond_dimensions": list(random.bond_dimensions),
        }, None
    if args.target == "bas":
        rows = 2 if args.rows is None else args.rows
        columns = 6 if args.columns is None else args.columns
        state = bars_and_stripes_state(rows, columns)
        return state, "bars_and_stripes", {
            "rows": rows,
            "columns": columns,
            "flattening": "row-major",
        }, None
    if args.target == "heisenberg-grid":
        rows = 3 if args.rows is None else args.rows
        columns = 4 if args.columns is None else args.columns
        hamiltonian = heisenberg_grid_hamiltonian(
            rows=rows, columns=columns, coupling=args.coupling
        )
        ground = exact_ground_state(hamiltonian, tolerance=args.eigensolver_tolerance)
        return ground.state, "heisenberg_grid_ground_state", {
            "rows": rows,
            "columns": columns,
            "coupling": args.coupling,
            "site_order": "row-major",
            "energy": ground.energy,
            "gap": ground.gap,
            "eigen_residual": ground.residual,
            "solver": ground.solver,
            "first_excited_energy": ground.first_excited_energy,
        }, hamiltonian
    if args.target == "tfim":
        hamiltonian = tfim_hamiltonian(
            args.num_qubits,
            coupling_zz=args.coupling,
            field_x=args.field_x,
            field_z=args.field_z,
        )
        ground = exact_ground_state(hamiltonian, tolerance=args.eigensolver_tolerance)
        return ground.state, "tfim_ground_state", {
            "num_qubits": args.num_qubits,
            "coupling_zz": args.coupling,
            "field_x": args.field_x,
            "field_z": args.field_z,
            "energy": ground.energy,
            "first_excited_energy": ground.first_excited_energy,
            "gap": ground.gap,
            "eigen_residual": ground.residual,
            "solver": ground.solver,
        }, hamiltonian
    if args.target == "xxz":
        hamiltonian = xxz_hamiltonian(
            args.num_qubits,
            delta=args.delta,
            coupling=args.coupling,
            field_z=args.field_z,
        )
        ground = exact_ground_state(hamiltonian, tolerance=args.eigensolver_tolerance)
        return ground.state, "xxz_ground_state", {
            "num_qubits": args.num_qubits,
            "delta": args.delta,
            "coupling": args.coupling,
            "field_z": args.field_z,
            "energy": ground.energy,
            "first_excited_energy": ground.first_excited_energy,
            "gap": ground.gap,
            "eigen_residual": ground.residual,
            "solver": ground.solver,
        }, hamiltonian
    raise AssertionError(f"Unhandled target {args.target!r}.")


def _run(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    target, name, metadata, hamiltonian = _build_target(args)
    result = run_iter_di_o_all(
        target,
        max_depth=args.depth,
        sweeps_per_depth=args.sweeps,
        learning_rate=args.learning_rate,
    )
    ground_energy = metadata.get("energy") if hamiltonian is not None else None
    manifest = save_reproduction_artifact(
        result,
        args.output,
        target_name=name,
        target_metadata=metadata,
        hamiltonian=hamiltonian,
        ground_energy=ground_energy,
    )
    verification = verify_reproduction_artifact(manifest.parent)
    summary = {
        "status": "completed",
        "manifest": str(manifest),
        "target": name,
        "depth": result.max_depth,
        "sweeps_per_depth": result.sweeps_per_depth,
        "total_gate_updates": result.total_gate_updates,
        "amplitude_fidelity": result.final_metrics.amplitude_fidelity,
        "paper_infidelity": result.final_metrics.paper_amplitude_infidelity,
        "squared_state_fidelity": result.final_metrics.squared_state_fidelity,
        "verification": verification,
    }
    if hamiltonian is not None and ground_energy is not None:
        summary["prepared_energy"] = energy_expectation(
            result.final_prepared_state, hamiltonian
        )
        summary["energy_error_per_site"] = energy_error_per_site(
            result.final_prepared_state, hamiltonian, ground_energy
        )
    print(json.dumps(summary, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))
    return 0


def _smoke(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    random = random_real_mps(4, max_bond=4, seed=args.seed)
    result = run_iter_di_o_all(
        random.state,
        max_depth=2,
        sweeps_per_depth=2,
        learning_rate=0.6,
    )
    manifest = save_reproduction_artifact(
        result,
        args.output,
        target_name="random_real_mps_smoke",
        target_metadata={"seed": args.seed, "num_qubits": 4, "max_bond": 4},
    )
    verification = verify_reproduction_artifact(manifest.parent)
    print(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "status": "completed",
                "manifest": str(manifest),
                "total_gate_updates": result.total_gate_updates,
                "amplitude_fidelity": result.final_metrics.amplitude_fidelity,
                "verification": verification,
            },
            indent=2,
            ensure_ascii=False,
        )
    )
    return 0


def _native_noise(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    exported = run_artifact_native_noise_protocol(
        args.artifact,
        args.output,
        physical_path=args.physical_path,
        optimization_level=args.optimization_level,
        seed_transpiler=args.seed_transpiler,
        seed_simulator=args.seed_simulator,
        max_density_matrix_qubits=args.max_density_matrix_qubits,
        compute_minimum_eigenvalue=args.compute_minimum_eigenvalue,
        compilation_fidelity_atol=args.compilation_fidelity_atol,
        gate_error=not args.disable_gate_error,
        thermal_relaxation=not args.disable_thermal_relaxation,
        temperature_millikelvin=args.temperature_millikelvin,
        provenance={"command": "native-noise", "doctor": doctor_snapshot()},
    )
    manifest = json.loads(exported.manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    noisy = manifest["noise_evaluation"]
    print(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "status": "completed",
                "manifest": str(exported.manifest_path),
                "manifest_sha256": exported.manifest_sha256,
                "backend_snapshot_sha256": manifest["noise_protocol"][
                    "backend_snapshot_sha256"
                ],
                "physical_path": manifest["noise_protocol"]["physical_path"],
                "logical_target_fidelity": noisy["fidelity"]["logical_target"],
                "compilation_fidelity": noisy["fidelity"]["compilation"],
                "noisy_target_fidelity": noisy["fidelity"]["noisy_target"],
                "noisy_native_retention_fidelity": noisy["fidelity"][
                    "noisy_native_retention"
                ],
                "energy_error_per_site": noisy["energy"]["error_per_site"],
            },
            indent=2,
            ensure_ascii=False,
        )
    )
    return 0


def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    """Build the complete CLI parser."""

    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="rudolph2023")
    subparsers = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)

    doctor = subparsers.add_parser("doctor", help="print numerical conventions")
    doctor.set_defaults(handler=lambda _args: print(json.dumps(doctor_snapshot(), indent=2)) or 0)

    smoke = subparsers.add_parser("smoke", help="run an audited N=4 end-to-end smoke")
    smoke.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
    smoke.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    smoke.set_defaults(handler=_smoke)

    run = subparsers.add_parser("run", help="run Iter[D_i O_all] on one target")
    run.add_argument(
        "--target",
        choices=("random-mps", "bas", "heisenberg-grid", "tfim", "xxz"),
        required=True,
    )
    run.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    run.add_argument("--depth", type=int, default=4)
    run.add_argument("--sweeps", type=int, default=10)
    run.add_argument("--learning-rate", type=float, default=0.6)
    run.add_argument("--num-qubits", type=int, default=12)
    run.add_argument("--max-bond", type=int, default=64)
    run.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
    run.add_argument(
        "--rows",
        type=int,
        default=None,
        help="target-specific default: BAS=2, Heisenberg grid=3",
    )
    run.add_argument(
        "--columns",
        type=int,
        default=None,
        help="target-specific default: BAS=6, Heisenberg grid=4",
    )
    run.add_argument("--coupling", type=float, default=1.0)
    run.add_argument("--field-x", type=float, default=1.0)
    run.add_argument("--field-z", type=float, default=0.0)
    run.add_argument("--delta", type=float, default=1.0)
    run.add_argument("--eigensolver-tolerance", type=float, default=1e-12)
    run.set_defaults(handler=_run)

    verify = subparsers.add_parser("verify-artifact", help="verify hashes and gate replay")
    verify.add_argument("artifact", type=Path)
    verify.set_defaults(
        handler=lambda args: print(
            json.dumps(verify_reproduction_artifact(args.artifact), indent=2)
        )
        or 0
    )

    noise = subparsers.add_parser(
        "native-noise",
        help="post-process one existing artifact with compact FakeWashingtonV2 noise",
    )
    noise.add_argument("--artifact", type=Path, required=True)
    noise.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    noise.add_argument("--physical-path", type=int, nargs="+", default=None)
    noise.add_argument("--optimization-level", type=int, choices=(0, 1, 2, 3), default=0)
    noise.add_argument("--seed-transpiler", type=int, default=2026)
    noise.add_argument("--seed-simulator", type=int, default=2026)
    noise.add_argument("--max-density-matrix-qubits", type=int, default=12)
    noise.add_argument("--compilation-fidelity-atol", type=float, default=2e-10)
    noise.add_argument("--temperature-millikelvin", type=float, default=0.0)
    noise.add_argument("--disable-gate-error", action="store_true")
    noise.add_argument("--disable-thermal-relaxation", action="store_true")
    eigenvalue = noise.add_mutually_exclusive_group()
    eigenvalue.add_argument(
        "--compute-minimum-eigenvalue",
        dest="compute_minimum_eigenvalue",
        action="store_true",
    )
    eigenvalue.add_argument(
        "--skip-minimum-eigenvalue",
        dest="compute_minimum_eigenvalue",
        action="store_false",
    )
    noise.set_defaults(
        compute_minimum_eigenvalue=None,
        handler=_native_noise,
    )
    return parser


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    """Run the CLI and return a process status."""

    args = build_parser().parse_args(argv)
    return int(args.handler(args))


__all__ = ["build_parser", "doctor_snapshot", "main"]

文件路径: src/rudolph2023/qiskit_adapter.py

"""Convention-safe Qiskit adapter for the Rudolph pure-U(4) staircase.

The paper reproduction itself deliberately has no Qiskit dependency.  Every
Qiskit import in this module is therefore lazy, so importing
``rudolph2023.qiskit_adapter`` remains safe in the NumPy/SciPy-only
environment.

The ordering bridge is

``project site i (q0 most significant) -> Qiskit qubit N - 1 - i``.

For a project-local U(4) whose basis is ``|left,right>`` this additionally
requires passing Qiskit qargs as ``[mapped_right, mapped_left]``.  With both
steps, Qiskit's statevector array has exactly the same computational-basis
ordering as the project's q0-MSB dense statevector; no bit reversal is needed.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import importlib
import importlib.util
import numbers
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any

import numpy as np
from numpy.typing import ArrayLike, NDArray

from .circuit import StaircaseCircuit

ComplexArray = NDArray[np.complex128]
NATIVE_BASIS = frozenset({"rz", "sx", "x", "cx"})


class QiskitUnavailableError(ImportError):
    """Raised when an explicitly requested optional Qiskit operation is unavailable."""


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class CompilationDiagnostics:
    """Ideal logical replay and exact native-synthesis diagnostics.

    All fidelities are normalized squared pure-state fidelities.  A phase-
    aligned error is the maximum absolute statevector difference after
    removing the candidate-over-reference global phase.
    """

    num_qubits: int
    depth: int
    num_u4_gates: int
    logical_replay_fidelity: float
    native_replay_fidelity: float
    compile_fidelity: float
    logical_phase_over_dense: float
    native_phase_over_logical: float
    logical_phase_aligned_max_abs_error: float
    native_phase_aligned_max_abs_error: float
    native_operation_counts: tuple[tuple[str, int], ...]
    native_basis_only: bool


def qiskit_available() -> bool:
    """Return whether a top-level :mod:`qiskit` package is discoverable."""

    try:
        return importlib.util.find_spec("qiskit") is not None
    except (ImportError, ModuleNotFoundError, ValueError):
        return False


def require_qiskit() -> Any:
    """Import Qiskit or raise one actionable optional-dependency error."""

    try:
        return importlib.import_module("qiskit")
    except (ImportError, ModuleNotFoundError) as exc:
        raise QiskitUnavailableError(
            "Qiskit is required for circuit translation and native synthesis; "
            "install the project's optional noise dependencies."
        ) from exc


def _require_staircase(circuit: StaircaseCircuit) -> StaircaseCircuit:
    if not isinstance(circuit, StaircaseCircuit):
        raise TypeError(
            "circuit must be a rudolph2023.circuit.StaircaseCircuit, "
            f"got {type(circuit).__name__}."
        )
    return circuit


def _validated_num_qubits(num_qubits: int) -> int:
    if isinstance(num_qubits, bool) or not isinstance(num_qubits, numbers.Integral):
        raise TypeError("num_qubits must be an integer.")
    result = int(num_qubits)
    if result < 2:
        raise ValueError("num_qubits must be at least two.")
    return result


def _validated_site(site: int, *, num_qubits: int) -> int:
    if isinstance(site, bool) or not isinstance(site, numbers.Integral):
        raise TypeError("site must be an integer.")
    result = int(site)
    if result < 0 or result >= num_qubits:
        raise ValueError(f"site={result} is outside an N={num_qubits} register.")
    return result


def project_site_to_qiskit(site: int, *, num_qubits: int) -> int:
    """Map one q0-MSB project site to its Qiskit little-endian qubit index."""

    width = _validated_num_qubits(num_qubits)
    validated = _validated_site(site, num_qubits=width)
    return width - 1 - validated


def project_sites_to_qiskit_qargs(
    sites: tuple[int, int],
    *,
    num_qubits: int,
) -> tuple[int, int]:
    """Map one ordered adjacent project pair while preserving its U(4) basis.

    Qiskit treats its first qarg as the least-significant local subsystem.
    The project lists the most-significant local site first, hence the
    intentional reversal after mapping the two project sites.
    """

    width = _validated_num_qubits(num_qubits)
    if not isinstance(sites, tuple):
        raise TypeError("sites must be a tuple containing one ordered adjacent pair.")
    if len(sites) != 2:
        raise ValueError("sites must contain exactly two sites.")
    left = _validated_site(sites[0], num_qubits=width)
    right = _validated_site(sites[1], num_qubits=width)
    if right != left + 1:
        raise ValueError("sites must be an ordered adjacent pair (left,left+1).")
    mapped = (
        project_site_to_qiskit(left, num_qubits=width),
        project_site_to_qiskit(right, num_qubits=width),
    )
    return mapped[1], mapped[0]


def to_qiskit_logical(
    circuit: StaircaseCircuit,
    *,
    insert_barriers: bool = False,
) -> Any:
    """Translate the physical U(4) execution order into exact Qiskit blocks."""

    circuit = _require_staircase(circuit)
    if not isinstance(insert_barriers, bool):
        raise TypeError("insert_barriers must be bool.")
    require_qiskit()
    from qiskit import QuantumCircuit

    translated = QuantumCircuit(circuit.num_qubits, name="rudolph2023_logical")
    for flat_index, gate in enumerate(circuit.flat_gates):
        qargs = project_sites_to_qiskit_qargs(
            (gate.left_site, gate.left_site + 1),
            num_qubits=circuit.num_qubits,
        )
        translated.unitary(
            np.asarray(gate.matrix, dtype=np.complex128),
            list(qargs),
            label=f"u4_l{gate.layer_index}_p{gate.position}",
        )
        if insert_barriers and flat_index + 1 < circuit.num_gates:
            translated.barrier()
    return translated


def remove_barriers(qiskit_circuit: Any) -> Any:
    """Return a circuit copy with barriers removed and global phase retained."""

    require_qiskit()
    from qiskit import QuantumCircuit
    from qiskit.transpiler.passes import RemoveBarriers

    if not isinstance(qiskit_circuit, QuantumCircuit):
        raise TypeError("qiskit_circuit must be a qiskit.QuantumCircuit.")
    return RemoveBarriers()(qiskit_circuit.copy())


def compile_staircase_native(
    circuit: StaircaseCircuit,
    *,
    insert_barriers: bool = False,
    remove_inserted_barriers: bool = True,
) -> Any:
    """Exactly synthesize every U(4) independently into ``rz/sx/x/cx``.

    Independent block synthesis preserves the Rudolph macro-layer resource
    boundary.  ``basis_fidelity=1`` and ``approximate=False`` forbid an
    approximate hardware-aware replacement at this stage.
    """

    circuit = _require_staircase(circuit)
    if not isinstance(insert_barriers, bool):
        raise TypeError("insert_barriers must be bool.")
    if not isinstance(remove_inserted_barriers, bool):
        raise TypeError("remove_inserted_barriers must be bool.")

    require_qiskit()
    from qiskit import QuantumCircuit
    from qiskit.circuit.library import CXGate
    try:
        from qiskit.synthesis import TwoQubitBasisDecomposer
    except ImportError:  # Qiskit 0.45 compatibility; Qiskit 2.x uses the path above.
        from qiskit.quantum_info.synthesis import TwoQubitBasisDecomposer

    decomposer = TwoQubitBasisDecomposer(
        CXGate(),
        basis_fidelity=1.0,
        euler_basis="ZSX",
        pulse_optimize=False,
    )
    native = QuantumCircuit(circuit.num_qubits, name="rudolph2023_native")
    for flat_index, gate in enumerate(circuit.flat_gates):
        block = decomposer(
            np.asarray(gate.matrix, dtype=np.complex128),
            basis_fidelity=1.0,
            approximate=False,
        )
        qargs = project_sites_to_qiskit_qargs(
            (gate.left_site, gate.left_site + 1),
            num_qubits=circuit.num_qubits,
        )
        native.compose(block, list(qargs), inplace=True)
        if insert_barriers and flat_index + 1 < circuit.num_gates:
            native.barrier()

    if insert_barriers and remove_inserted_barriers:
        native = remove_barriers(native)
    unexpected = set(native.count_ops()) - NATIVE_BASIS - {"barrier"}
    if unexpected:
        joined = ", ".join(sorted(unexpected))
        raise RuntimeError(f"Exact synthesis emitted non-native operations: {joined}.")
    return native


def qiskit_statevector_q0_msb(qiskit_circuit: Any) -> ComplexArray:
    """Ideally execute a Qiskit circuit in the project's direct q0-MSB array order."""

    require_qiskit()
    from qiskit import QuantumCircuit
    from qiskit.quantum_info import Statevector

    if not isinstance(qiskit_circuit, QuantumCircuit):
        raise TypeError("qiskit_circuit must be a qiskit.QuantumCircuit.")
    state = np.asarray(Statevector.from_instruction(qiskit_circuit).data, dtype=np.complex128)
    if state.shape != (1 << qiskit_circuit.num_qubits,):
        raise ArithmeticError("Qiskit returned an unexpected statevector shape.")
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(state)):
        raise ArithmeticError("Qiskit returned a non-finite statevector.")
    return np.array(state, dtype=np.complex128, copy=True)


def statevector_fidelity(reference: ArrayLike, candidate: ArrayLike) -> float:
    """Return normalized squared pure-state fidelity without numerical clipping."""

    reference_array = np.asarray(reference, dtype=np.complex128).reshape(-1)
    candidate_array = np.asarray(candidate, dtype=np.complex128).reshape(-1)
    if reference_array.shape != candidate_array.shape:
        raise ValueError(
            "Statevectors must have the same shape; "
            f"got {reference_array.shape} and {candidate_array.shape}."
        )
    if reference_array.size == 0:
        raise ValueError("Statevectors must not be empty.")
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(reference_array)) or not np.all(np.isfinite(candidate_array)):
        raise ValueError("Statevectors must contain only finite values.")
    reference_norm_sq = float(np.vdot(reference_array, reference_array).real)
    candidate_norm_sq = float(np.vdot(candidate_array, candidate_array).real)
    if reference_norm_sq <= 0.0 or candidate_norm_sq <= 0.0:
        raise ValueError("Statevectors must have nonzero norm.")
    overlap = complex(np.vdot(reference_array, candidate_array))
    return float(abs(overlap) ** 2 / (reference_norm_sq * candidate_norm_sq))


def _phase_aligned_comparison(
    reference: ArrayLike,
    candidate: ArrayLike,
) -> tuple[float, float]:
    reference_array = np.asarray(reference, dtype=np.complex128).reshape(-1)
    candidate_array = np.asarray(candidate, dtype=np.complex128).reshape(-1)
    # Reuse the strict validation, including equal shape and nonzero norms.
    statevector_fidelity(reference_array, candidate_array)
    reference_unit = reference_array / np.linalg.norm(reference_array)
    candidate_unit = candidate_array / np.linalg.norm(candidate_array)
    overlap = complex(np.vdot(reference_unit, candidate_unit))
    phase = float(np.angle(overlap)) if abs(overlap) > 0.0 else 0.0
    aligned = candidate_unit * np.exp(-1j * phase)
    return phase, float(np.max(np.abs(reference_unit - aligned)))


def diagnose_qiskit_compilation(
    circuit: StaircaseCircuit,
    native_circuit: Any | None = None,
) -> CompilationDiagnostics:
    """Cross-check project-dense, logical-Qiskit, and native-Qiskit replays."""

    circuit = _require_staircase(circuit)
    logical_circuit = to_qiskit_logical(circuit)
    if native_circuit is None:
        native_circuit = compile_staircase_native(circuit)

    require_qiskit()
    from qiskit import QuantumCircuit

    if not isinstance(native_circuit, QuantumCircuit):
        raise TypeError("native_circuit must be a qiskit.QuantumCircuit.")
    if native_circuit.num_qubits != circuit.num_qubits:
        raise ValueError(
            "native_circuit qubit count does not match staircase: "
            f"{native_circuit.num_qubits} != {circuit.num_qubits}."
        )

    dense_state = circuit.prepare()
    logical_state = qiskit_statevector_q0_msb(logical_circuit)
    native_state = qiskit_statevector_q0_msb(native_circuit)
    logical_phase, logical_error = _phase_aligned_comparison(dense_state, logical_state)
    native_phase, native_error = _phase_aligned_comparison(logical_state, native_state)
    counts = tuple(
        sorted((str(name), int(count)) for name, count in native_circuit.count_ops().items())
    )
    operation_names = {name for name, _ in counts}
    return CompilationDiagnostics(
        num_qubits=circuit.num_qubits,
        depth=circuit.depth,
        num_u4_gates=circuit.num_gates,
        logical_replay_fidelity=statevector_fidelity(dense_state, logical_state),
        native_replay_fidelity=statevector_fidelity(dense_state, native_state),
        compile_fidelity=statevector_fidelity(logical_state, native_state),
        logical_phase_over_dense=logical_phase,
        native_phase_over_logical=native_phase,
        logical_phase_aligned_max_abs_error=logical_error,
        native_phase_aligned_max_abs_error=native_error,
        native_operation_counts=counts,
        native_basis_only=(operation_names - {"barrier"}) <= NATIVE_BASIS,
    )


__all__ = [
    "CompilationDiagnostics",
    "NATIVE_BASIS",
    "QiskitUnavailableError",
    "compile_staircase_native",
    "diagnose_qiskit_compilation",
    "project_site_to_qiskit",
    "project_sites_to_qiskit_qargs",
    "qiskit_available",
    "qiskit_statevector_q0_msb",
    "remove_barriers",
    "require_qiskit",
    "statevector_fidelity",
    "to_qiskit_logical",
]

文件路径: src/rudolph2023/conventions.py

"""Numerical, basis, and global-phase conventions for the reproduction."""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
import numbers

import numpy as np
from numpy.typing import ArrayLike, NDArray

COMPLEX_DTYPE = np.dtype(np.complex128)
REAL_DTYPE = np.dtype(np.float64)
QUBIT_ORDER = "q0-most-significant"
TWO_QUBIT_BASIS = ("00", "01", "10", "11")
MPS_AXIS_ORDER = ("chi_left", "physical", "chi_right")
LAYER_GATE_ORDER = "left-to-right adjacent U4: (0,1),...,(N-2,N-1)"


def as_complex128(array: ArrayLike, *, copy: bool = False) -> NDArray[np.complex128]:
    """Return an array in the only supported complex dtype."""

    if copy:
        result = np.array(array, dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE, copy=True)
    else:
        result = np.asarray(array, dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(result)):
        raise ValueError("Array must contain only finite values.")
    return result


def validate_integer(value: int, *, name: str, minimum: int) -> int:
    """Validate an integer while rejecting booleans explicitly."""

    if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, numbers.Integral):
        raise TypeError(f"{name} must be an integer.")
    result = int(value)
    if result < minimum:
        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be at least {minimum}, got {result}.")
    return result


def validate_real(
    value: float,
    *,
    name: str,
    minimum: float | None = None,
    maximum: float | None = None,
) -> float:
    """Validate a finite real scalar and optional closed bounds."""

    if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, numbers.Real):
        raise TypeError(f"{name} must be a real scalar.")
    result = float(value)
    if not math.isfinite(result):
        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be finite.")
    if minimum is not None and result < minimum:
        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be >= {minimum}, got {result}.")
    if maximum is not None and result > maximum:
        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be <= {maximum}, got {result}.")
    return result


def infer_num_qubits(state: ArrayLike) -> int:
    """Infer N from a nonempty power-of-two statevector length."""

    size = int(np.asarray(state).size)
    if size < 2 or size & (size - 1):
        raise ValueError(f"Statevector length must be a power of two >= 2, got {size}.")
    return size.bit_length() - 1


def normalized_statevector(state: ArrayLike) -> NDArray[np.complex128]:
    """Return a normalized finite statevector without fixing global phase."""

    vector = as_complex128(state, copy=True).reshape(-1)
    infer_num_qubits(vector)
    norm = float(np.linalg.norm(vector))
    if not math.isfinite(norm) or norm <= 0.0:
        raise ValueError("Statevector must have a finite nonzero norm.")
    return vector / norm


def validate_normalized_statevector(
    state: ArrayLike,
    *,
    atol: float = 1e-12,
) -> NDArray[np.complex128]:
    """Validate, but do not silently normalize, a unit-norm statevector."""

    atol = validate_real(atol, name="atol", minimum=0.0)
    vector = as_complex128(state, copy=True).reshape(-1)
    infer_num_qubits(vector)
    norm = float(np.linalg.norm(vector))
    if abs(norm - 1.0) > atol:
        raise ValueError(f"Statevector must be normalized; observed norm={norm:.17g}.")
    return vector


def phase_fix_state(state: ArrayLike) -> NDArray[np.complex128]:
    """Fix global phase at the first maximum-modulus amplitude.

    Rudolph's fractional principal-branch update is not invariant under an
    arbitrary target global phase.  This physical gauge makes the protocol
    deterministic: ties use the smallest computational-basis index.
    """

    vector = normalized_statevector(state)
    pivot = int(np.argmax(np.abs(vector)))
    magnitude = abs(vector[pivot])
    if magnitude == 0.0:  # unreachable for a normalized vector
        raise FloatingPointError("Cannot phase-fix a zero statevector.")
    vector *= np.exp(-1j * np.angle(vector[pivot]))
    vector[pivot] = complex(abs(vector[pivot]), 0.0)
    return vector


def phase_aligned_state(reference: ArrayLike, candidate: ArrayLike) -> NDArray[np.complex128]:
    """Align a candidate to a reference for diagnostic comparisons only."""

    ref = normalized_statevector(reference)
    cand = normalized_statevector(candidate)
    if ref.shape != cand.shape:
        raise ValueError(f"Statevector shape mismatch: {ref.shape} vs {cand.shape}.")
    overlap = np.vdot(ref, cand)
    if abs(overlap) > 0.0:
        cand *= np.exp(-1j * np.angle(overlap))
    return cand


def state_distance_up_to_global_phase(reference: ArrayLike, candidate: ArrayLike) -> float:
    """Return normalized L2 distance after optimal global-phase alignment."""

    ref = normalized_statevector(reference)
    return float(np.linalg.norm(ref - phase_aligned_state(ref, candidate)))


__all__ = [
    "COMPLEX_DTYPE",
    "LAYER_GATE_ORDER",
    "MPS_AXIS_ORDER",
    "QUBIT_ORDER",
    "REAL_DTYPE",
    "TWO_QUBIT_BASIS",
    "as_complex128",
    "infer_num_qubits",
    "normalized_statevector",
    "phase_aligned_state",
    "phase_fix_state",
    "state_distance_up_to_global_phase",
    "validate_integer",
    "validate_normalized_statevector",
    "validate_real",
]

文件路径: src/rudolph2023/native_noise_protocol.py
