"""Rudolph environment/polar forward sweeps with equation-(6) damping."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

import numpy as np
from numpy.typing import ArrayLike, NDArray

from .circuit import (
    StaircaseCircuit,
    apply_adjacent_u4,
    zero_state,
)
from .conventions import (
    COMPLEX_DTYPE,
    as_complex128,
    infer_num_qubits,
    validate_integer,
    validate_normalized_statevector,
    validate_real,
)
from .linalg import damped_unitary_update, polar_optimal_unitary

ComplexArray = NDArray[np.complex128]


def local_state_matrix(
    state: ArrayLike,
    left_site: int,
    *,
    num_qubits: int,
) -> ComplexArray:
    """Reshape a state as ``(local 00/01/10/11, all other qubits)``."""

    vector = as_complex128(state, copy=True).reshape(-1)
    num_qubits = validate_integer(num_qubits, name="num_qubits", minimum=2)
    if infer_num_qubits(vector) != num_qubits:
        raise ValueError("State size does not match num_qubits.")
    left_site = validate_integer(left_site, name="left_site", minimum=0)
    if left_site >= num_qubits - 1:
        raise IndexError("Adjacent pair is outside the register.")
    tensor = vector.reshape((2,) * num_qubits)
    leading = np.moveaxis(tensor, (left_site, left_site + 1), (0, 1))
    return np.asarray(leading.reshape(4, -1), dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE)


def build_suffix_states(
    target_state: ArrayLike,
    circuit: StaircaseCircuit,
) -> tuple[ComplexArray, ...]:
    """Return suffix[k] with future gates k..M-1 removed by adjoints."""

    if not isinstance(circuit, StaircaseCircuit):
        raise TypeError("circuit must be StaircaseCircuit.")
    target = validate_normalized_statevector(target_state)
    if infer_num_qubits(target) != circuit.num_qubits:
        raise ValueError("Target width differs from circuit width.")
    gates = circuit.flat_gates
    suffixes: list[ComplexArray | None] = [None] * (len(gates) + 1)
    suffixes[-1] = target
    running = target
    for index in range(len(gates) - 1, -1, -1):
        gate = gates[index]
        running = apply_adjacent_u4(
            running,
            gate.matrix.conj().T,
            gate.left_site,
            num_qubits=circuit.num_qubits,
            validate_matrix=False,
        )
        suffixes[index] = running
    if any(state is None for state in suffixes):
        raise AssertionError("Suffix construction left an empty entry.")
    return tuple(state for state in suffixes if state is not None)


def gate_environment(
    prefix_state: ArrayLike,
    suffix_state: ArrayLike,
    *,
    left_site: int,
    num_qubits: int,
) -> ComplexArray:
    """Return E such that ``Tr(E^dagger U)=<suffix|U|prefix>``."""

    prefix_local = local_state_matrix(
        prefix_state, left_site, num_qubits=num_qubits
    )
    suffix_local = local_state_matrix(
        suffix_state, left_site, num_qubits=num_qubits
    )
    return np.asarray(suffix_local @ prefix_local.conj().T, dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE)


def environment_overlap_identity(
    environment: ArrayLike,
    gate: ArrayLike,
    prefix_state: ArrayLike,
    suffix_state: ArrayLike,
    *,
    left_site: int,
    num_qubits: int,
) -> tuple[complex, complex, float]:
    """Evaluate the environment identity through two independent paths."""

    environment_matrix = as_complex128(environment)
    gate_matrix = as_complex128(gate)
    via_environment = complex(np.vdot(environment_matrix, gate_matrix))
    local_output = apply_adjacent_u4(
        prefix_state,
        gate_matrix,
        left_site,
        num_qubits=num_qubits,
        validate_matrix=False,
    )
    via_state = complex(np.vdot(as_complex128(suffix_state), local_output))
    return via_environment, via_state, float(abs(via_environment - via_state))


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class GateUpdateRecord:
    """Mathematical audit trail for one gate update."""

    sweep_index: int
    flat_gate_index: int
    layer_index: int
    position: int
    left_site: int
    overlap_before: complex
    overlap_after: complex
    amplitude_before: float
    amplitude_after: float
    squared_fidelity_before: float
    squared_fidelity_after: float
    amplitude_gain: float
    squared_fidelity_gain: float
    environment_identity_error_before: float
    environment_identity_error_after: float
    environment_nuclear_norm: float
    environment_rank: int
    polar_unitarity_residual: float
    updated_unitarity_residual: float
    learning_rate: float


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class SweepRecord:
    """One complete forward Gauss-Seidel sweep."""

    sweep_index: int
    overlap_before: complex
    overlap_after: complex
    amplitude_fidelity_before: float
    amplitude_fidelity_after: float
    squared_fidelity_before: float
    squared_fidelity_after: float
    max_environment_identity_error: float
    max_unitarity_residual: float
    replay_error: float
    gate_updates: tuple[GateUpdateRecord, ...]

    @property
    def amplitude_gain(self) -> float:
        return self.amplitude_fidelity_after - self.amplitude_fidelity_before

    @property
    def squared_fidelity_gain(self) -> float:
        return self.squared_fidelity_after - self.squared_fidelity_before


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class OptimizationResult:
    """Fixed-budget optimization result and full sweep trajectory."""

    initial_circuit: StaircaseCircuit
    optimized_circuit: StaircaseCircuit
    learning_rate: float
    requested_sweeps: int
    sweeps: tuple[SweepRecord, ...]
    initial_overlap: complex
    final_overlap: complex
    initial_amplitude_fidelity: float
    final_amplitude_fidelity: float
    initial_squared_fidelity: float
    final_squared_fidelity: float

    @property
    def total_gate_updates(self) -> int:
        return sum(len(sweep.gate_updates) for sweep in self.sweeps)


def forward_sweep(
    target_state: ArrayLike,
    circuit: StaircaseCircuit,
    *,
    learning_rate: float = 0.6,
    sweep_index: int = 0,
    environment_atol: float = 1e-11,
) -> tuple[StaircaseCircuit, SweepRecord]:
    """Perform one cached forward sweep exactly following paper Algorithm 2.

    The damped principal-branch update is not universally monotone for an
    arbitrary environment.  Gains are therefore recorded, not asserted.
    """

    if not isinstance(circuit, StaircaseCircuit):
        raise TypeError("circuit must be StaircaseCircuit.")
    sweep_index = validate_integer(sweep_index, name="sweep_index", minimum=0)
    rate = validate_real(
        learning_rate, name="learning_rate", minimum=0.0, maximum=1.0
    )
    environment_atol = validate_real(
        environment_atol, name="environment_atol", minimum=0.0
    )
    target = validate_normalized_statevector(target_state)
    if infer_num_qubits(target) != circuit.num_qubits:
        raise ValueError("Target width differs from circuit width.")

    old_gates = circuit.flat_gates
    suffixes = build_suffix_states(target, circuit)
    initial_prepared = circuit.prepare()
    overlap_before = complex(np.vdot(target, initial_prepared))
    prefix = zero_state(circuit.num_qubits)
    updated_matrices: list[ComplexArray] = []
    records: list[GateUpdateRecord] = []

    for flat_index, old_gate in enumerate(old_gates):
        suffix = suffixes[flat_index + 1]
        environment = gate_environment(
            prefix,
            suffix,
            left_site=old_gate.left_site,
            num_qubits=circuit.num_qubits,
        )
        env_before, direct_before, error_before = environment_overlap_identity(
            environment,
            old_gate.matrix,
            prefix,
            suffix,
            left_site=old_gate.left_site,
            num_qubits=circuit.num_qubits,
        )
        if error_before > environment_atol * max(1.0, abs(direct_before)):
            raise ArithmeticError(
                f"Environment identity failed before gate {flat_index}: {error_before:.3e}."
            )

        polar = polar_optimal_unitary(environment)
        damped = damped_unitary_update(
            old_gate.matrix,
            polar.unitary,
            learning_rate=rate,
        )
        env_after, direct_after, error_after = environment_overlap_identity(
            environment,
            damped.unitary,
            prefix,
            suffix,
            left_site=old_gate.left_site,
            num_qubits=circuit.num_qubits,
        )
        if error_after > environment_atol * max(1.0, abs(direct_after)):
            raise ArithmeticError(
                f"Environment identity failed after gate {flat_index}: {error_after:.3e}."
            )

        prefix = apply_adjacent_u4(
            prefix,
            damped.unitary,
            old_gate.left_site,
            num_qubits=circuit.num_qubits,
            validate_matrix=False,
        )
        updated_matrices.append(damped.unitary)
        amplitude_before = float(abs(direct_before))
        amplitude_after = float(abs(direct_after))
        records.append(
            GateUpdateRecord(
                sweep_index=sweep_index,
                flat_gate_index=flat_index,
                layer_index=old_gate.layer_index,
                position=old_gate.position,
                left_site=old_gate.left_site,
                overlap_before=env_before,
                overlap_after=env_after,
                amplitude_before=amplitude_before,
                amplitude_after=amplitude_after,
                squared_fidelity_before=amplitude_before**2,
                squared_fidelity_after=amplitude_after**2,
                amplitude_gain=amplitude_after - amplitude_before,
                squared_fidelity_gain=amplitude_after**2 - amplitude_before**2,
                environment_identity_error_before=error_before,
                environment_identity_error_after=error_after,
                environment_nuclear_norm=polar.nuclear_norm,
                environment_rank=polar.numerical_rank,
                polar_unitarity_residual=polar.unitarity_residual,
                updated_unitarity_residual=damped.unitarity_residual,
                learning_rate=rate,
            )
        )

    optimized = circuit.with_flat_matrices(updated_matrices)
    overlap_after = complex(np.vdot(target, prefix))
    replayed = optimized.prepare()
    replay_error = float(np.max(np.abs(prefix - replayed)))
    if replay_error > environment_atol:
        raise ArithmeticError(f"End-of-sweep cached prefix replay failed: {replay_error:.3e}.")
    direct_final_overlap = complex(np.vdot(target, replayed))
    if abs(overlap_after - direct_final_overlap) > environment_atol:
        raise ArithmeticError("End-of-sweep overlap replay failed.")

    max_environment_error = max(
        (
            max(record.environment_identity_error_before, record.environment_identity_error_after)
            for record in records
        ),
        default=0.0,
    )
    max_unitarity = max(
        (record.updated_unitarity_residual for record in records), default=0.0
    )
    record = SweepRecord(
        sweep_index=sweep_index,
        overlap_before=overlap_before,
        overlap_after=overlap_after,
        amplitude_fidelity_before=float(abs(overlap_before)),
        amplitude_fidelity_after=float(abs(overlap_after)),
        squared_fidelity_before=float(abs(overlap_before) ** 2),
        squared_fidelity_after=float(abs(overlap_after) ** 2),
        max_environment_identity_error=max_environment_error,
        max_unitarity_residual=max_unitarity,
        replay_error=replay_error,
        gate_updates=tuple(records),
    )
    return optimized, record


def optimize_all(
    target_state: ArrayLike,
    initial_circuit: StaircaseCircuit,
    *,
    sweeps: int,
    learning_rate: float = 0.6,
    environment_atol: float = 1e-11,
) -> OptimizationResult:
    """Run an exact fixed number of forward sweeps; no convergence early stop."""

    if not isinstance(initial_circuit, StaircaseCircuit):
        raise TypeError("initial_circuit must be StaircaseCircuit.")
    sweeps = validate_integer(sweeps, name="sweeps", minimum=0)
    rate = validate_real(
        learning_rate, name="learning_rate", minimum=0.0, maximum=1.0
    )
    target = validate_normalized_statevector(target_state)
    initial_overlap = complex(np.vdot(target, initial_circuit.prepare()))
    circuit = initial_circuit
    records: list[SweepRecord] = []
    for sweep_index in range(sweeps):
        circuit, record = forward_sweep(
            target,
            circuit,
            learning_rate=rate,
            sweep_index=sweep_index,
            environment_atol=environment_atol,
        )
        records.append(record)
    final_overlap = complex(np.vdot(target, circuit.prepare()))
    return OptimizationResult(
        initial_circuit=initial_circuit,
        optimized_circuit=circuit,
        learning_rate=rate,
        requested_sweeps=sweeps,
        sweeps=tuple(records),
        initial_overlap=initial_overlap,
        final_overlap=final_overlap,
        initial_amplitude_fidelity=float(abs(initial_overlap)),
        final_amplitude_fidelity=float(abs(final_overlap)),
        initial_squared_fidelity=float(abs(initial_overlap) ** 2),
        final_squared_fidelity=float(abs(final_overlap) ** 2),
    )


__all__ = [
    "GateUpdateRecord",
    "OptimizationResult",
    "SweepRecord",
    "build_suffix_states",
    "environment_overlap_identity",
    "forward_sweep",
    "gate_environment",
    "local_state_matrix",
    "optimize_all",
]

"""Deterministic isometry-to-unitary completion for the Ran analytic layer."""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
import numbers
from collections.abc import Sequence

import numpy as np
from numpy.typing import ArrayLike, NDArray

from .conventions import COMPLEX_DTYPE, as_complex128
from .linalg import unitarity_residual


def _double_mgs(
    vector: NDArray[np.complex128],
    basis: Sequence[NDArray[np.complex128]],
) -> NDArray[np.complex128]:
    residual = vector.copy()
    for _ in range(2):
        for column in basis:
            residual -= column * np.vdot(column, residual)
    return residual


def complete_unitary_from_columns(
    prescribed_columns: ArrayLike,
    column_indices: Sequence[int],
    *,
    dimension: int | None = None,
    atol: float = 1e-12,
) -> NDArray[np.complex128]:
    """Complete fixed orthonormal columns using computational-basis double MGS."""

    prescribed = as_complex128(prescribed_columns, copy=True)
    if prescribed.ndim != 2:
        raise ValueError("prescribed_columns must be a matrix.")
    inferred_dimension, count = prescribed.shape
    if dimension is None:
        dimension = inferred_dimension
    else:
        if isinstance(dimension, bool) or not isinstance(dimension, numbers.Integral):
            raise TypeError("dimension must be an integer or None.")
        dimension = int(dimension)
    if dimension < 1 or inferred_dimension != dimension:
        raise ValueError("Invalid completion dimension.")
    if not math.isfinite(atol) or atol <= 0.0:
        raise ValueError("atol must be finite and positive.")
    raw_indices = tuple(column_indices)
    if any(
        isinstance(index, bool) or not isinstance(index, numbers.Integral)
        for index in raw_indices
    ):
        raise TypeError("column_indices must contain only integers.")
    indices = tuple(int(index) for index in raw_indices)
    if len(indices) != count or len(set(indices)) != count:
        raise ValueError("column_indices must uniquely match prescribed columns.")
    if any(index < 0 or index >= dimension for index in indices):
        raise ValueError("column_indices are outside the unitary dimension.")
    gram = prescribed.conj().T @ prescribed
    if not np.allclose(gram, np.eye(count), atol=atol, rtol=0.0):
        raise ValueError("Prescribed columns are not orthonormal.")

    filled = {index: prescribed[:, offset].copy() for offset, index in enumerate(indices)}
    basis = [prescribed[:, offset].copy() for offset in range(count)]
    missing = [index for index in range(dimension) if index not in filled]
    missing_offset = 0
    for anchor_index in range(dimension):
        if missing_offset == len(missing):
            break
        anchor = np.zeros(dimension, dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE)
        anchor[anchor_index] = 1.0
        residual = _double_mgs(anchor, basis)
        norm = float(np.linalg.norm(residual))
        if norm <= atol:
            continue
        column = residual / norm
        pivot = int(np.argmax(np.abs(column)))
        column *= np.exp(-1j * np.angle(column[pivot]))
        filled[missing[missing_offset]] = column
        basis.append(column)
        missing_offset += 1
    if missing_offset != len(missing):
        raise np.linalg.LinAlgError("Could not span the unitary complement.")

    result = np.column_stack([filled[index] for index in range(dimension)])
    if unitarity_residual(result) > 32.0 * atol:
        raise ArithmeticError("Completed matrix is not unitary.")
    # Prescribed columns must remain bitwise/numerically unchanged.
    for offset, index in enumerate(indices):
        if not np.array_equal(result[:, index], prescribed[:, offset]):
            raise ArithmeticError("Completion changed a prescribed column.")
    return np.asarray(result, dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE)


__all__ = ["complete_unitary_from_columns"]



========================================================================
文件路径: src/rudolph2023/targets.py
========================================================================
"""Paper targets and additional many-body ground states for encoding tests."""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass

import numpy as np
from numpy.typing import ArrayLike, NDArray
from scipy.linalg import eigh
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix, isspmatrix, kron
from scipy.sparse.linalg import eigsh

from .conventions import (
    COMPLEX_DTYPE,
    phase_fix_state,
    validate_integer,
    validate_real,
)
from .mps import (
    MPS,
    canonicalize_to_ran,
    mps_to_dense,
    normalize_mps,
    open_boundary_bond_dimensions,
)

ComplexArray = NDArray[np.complex128]

PAULI_I = np.eye(2, dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE)
PAULI_X = np.array([[0.0, 1.0], [1.0, 0.0]], dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE)
PAULI_Y = np.array([[0.0, -1j], [1j, 0.0]], dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE)
PAULI_Z = np.array([[1.0, 0.0], [0.0, -1.0]], dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE)
SPIN_X = PAULI_X / 2.0
SPIN_Y = PAULI_Y / 2.0
SPIN_Z = PAULI_Z / 2.0


def grid_edges(rows: int, columns: int) -> tuple[tuple[int, int], ...]:
    """Return row-major open-boundary horizontal then vertical grid edges."""

    rows = validate_integer(rows, name="rows", minimum=1)
    columns = validate_integer(columns, name="columns", minimum=1)
    edges: list[tuple[int, int]] = []
    for row in range(rows):
        for column in range(columns - 1):
            site = row * columns + column
            edges.append((site, site + 1))
    for row in range(rows - 1):
        for column in range(columns):
            site = row * columns + column
            edges.append((site, site + columns))
    if len(set(edges)) != len(edges) or any(left == right for left, right in edges):
        raise AssertionError("Grid edge construction produced duplicates or self loops.")
    return tuple(edges)


def single_site_operator(
    operator: ArrayLike,
    site: int,
    num_qubits: int,
) -> csr_matrix:
    """Embed a 2x2 operator at one q0-MSB site as a sparse matrix."""

    num_qubits = validate_integer(num_qubits, name="num_qubits", minimum=1)
    site = validate_integer(site, name="site", minimum=0)
    if site >= num_qubits:
        raise IndexError("site is outside the register.")
    local = np.asarray(operator, dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE)
    if local.shape != (2, 2) or not np.all(np.isfinite(local)):
        raise ValueError("operator must be a finite 2x2 matrix.")
    result = csr_matrix([[1.0 + 0.0j]])
    for index in range(num_qubits):
        factor = csr_matrix(local if index == site else PAULI_I)
        result = kron(result, factor, format="csr")
    return result


def two_site_product_operator(
    first_operator: ArrayLike,
    second_operator: ArrayLike,
    first_site: int,
    second_site: int,
    num_qubits: int,
) -> csr_matrix:
    """Embed (A_i B_j) at any two distinct q0-MSB sites."""

    num_qubits = validate_integer(num_qubits, name="num_qubits", minimum=2)
    first_site = validate_integer(first_site, name="first_site", minimum=0)
    second_site = validate_integer(second_site, name="second_site", minimum=0)
    if first_site >= num_qubits or second_site >= num_qubits:
        raise IndexError("Operator site is outside the register.")
    if first_site == second_site:
        raise ValueError("Two-site product requires distinct sites.")
    first = np.asarray(first_operator, dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE)
    second = np.asarray(second_operator, dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE)
    if (
        first.shape != (2, 2)
        or second.shape != (2, 2)
        or not np.all(np.isfinite(first))
        or not np.all(np.isfinite(second))
    ):
        raise ValueError("Local operators must be finite 2x2 matrices.")
    result = csr_matrix([[1.0 + 0.0j]])
    for site in range(num_qubits):
        if site == first_site:
            local = first
        elif site == second_site:
            local = second
        else:
            local = PAULI_I
        result = kron(result, csr_matrix(local), format="csr")
    return result


def heisenberg_grid_hamiltonian(
    *,
    rows: int = 3,
    columns: int = 4,
    coupling: float = 1.0,
) -> csr_matrix:
    """Return the paper's OBC AFM grid H using spin operators Pauli/2."""

    rows = validate_integer(rows, name="rows", minimum=1)
    columns = validate_integer(columns, name="columns", minimum=1)
    if rows * columns < 2:
        raise ValueError("Heisenberg grid needs at least two sites.")
    coupling = validate_real(coupling, name="coupling")
    num_qubits = rows * columns
    dimension = 1 << num_qubits
    hamiltonian = csr_matrix((dimension, dimension), dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE)
    for first, second in grid_edges(rows, columns):
        for spin in (SPIN_X, SPIN_Y, SPIN_Z):
            hamiltonian += coupling * two_site_product_operator(
                spin, spin, first, second, num_qubits
            )
    hamiltonian = hamiltonian.tocsr()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(hamiltonian.data)):
        raise FloatingPointError("Heisenberg Hamiltonian contains non-finite values.")
    return hamiltonian


def tfim_hamiltonian(
    num_qubits: int,
    *,
    coupling_zz: float = 1.0,
    field_x: float = 1.0,
    field_z: float = 0.0,
) -> csr_matrix:
    """Return the OBC transverse-field Ising Hamiltonian."""

    num_qubits = validate_integer(num_qubits, name="num_qubits", minimum=2)
    coupling_zz = validate_real(coupling_zz, name="coupling_zz")
    field_x = validate_real(field_x, name="field_x")
    field_z = validate_real(field_z, name="field_z")
    dimension = 1 << num_qubits
    hamiltonian = csr_matrix((dimension, dimension), dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE)
    for site in range(num_qubits - 1):
        hamiltonian -= coupling_zz * two_site_product_operator(
            PAULI_Z, PAULI_Z, site, site + 1, num_qubits
        )
    for site in range(num_qubits):
        hamiltonian -= field_x * single_site_operator(PAULI_X, site, num_qubits)
        if field_z != 0.0:
            hamiltonian -= field_z * single_site_operator(PAULI_Z, site, num_qubits)
    hamiltonian = hamiltonian.tocsr()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(hamiltonian.data)):
        raise FloatingPointError("TFIM Hamiltonian contains non-finite values.")
    return hamiltonian


def xxz_hamiltonian(
    num_qubits: int,
    *,
    delta: float = 1.0,
    coupling: float = 1.0,
    field_z: float = 0.0,
) -> csr_matrix:
    """Return the OBC spin-one-half XXZ Hamiltonian with optional z field."""

    num_qubits = validate_integer(num_qubits, name="num_qubits", minimum=2)
    delta = validate_real(delta, name="delta")
    coupling = validate_real(coupling, name="coupling")
    field_z = validate_real(field_z, name="field_z")
    dimension = 1 << num_qubits
    hamiltonian = csr_matrix((dimension, dimension), dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE)
    for site in range(num_qubits - 1):
        hamiltonian += coupling * two_site_product_operator(
            SPIN_X, SPIN_X, site, site + 1, num_qubits
        )
        hamiltonian += coupling * two_site_product_operator(
            SPIN_Y, SPIN_Y, site, site + 1, num_qubits
        )
        hamiltonian += coupling * delta * two_site_product_operator(
            SPIN_Z, SPIN_Z, site, site + 1, num_qubits
        )
    if field_z != 0.0:
        for site in range(num_qubits):
            hamiltonian += field_z * single_site_operator(SPIN_Z, site, num_qubits)
    hamiltonian = hamiltonian.tocsr()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(hamiltonian.data)):
        raise FloatingPointError("XXZ Hamiltonian contains non-finite values.")
    return hamiltonian


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class GroundStateResult:
    """Exact/sparse-ED ground-state diagnostics."""

    state: ComplexArray
    energy: float
    first_excited_energy: float
    gap: float
    residual: float
    solver: str


def exact_ground_state(
    hamiltonian: ArrayLike | csr_matrix,
    *,
    tolerance: float = 1e-12,
    dense_dimension_cutoff: int = 512,
) -> GroundStateResult:
    """Return a deterministic phase-fixed ground state with residual check."""

    tolerance = validate_real(tolerance, name="tolerance", minimum=0.0)
    dense_dimension_cutoff = validate_integer(
        dense_dimension_cutoff, name="dense_dimension_cutoff", minimum=2
    )
    shape = getattr(hamiltonian, "shape", None)
    if not isinstance(shape, tuple) or len(shape) != 2 or shape[0] != shape[1]:
        raise ValueError("Hamiltonian must be square.")
    dimension = int(shape[0])
    if dimension < 2 or dimension & (dimension - 1):
        raise ValueError("Hamiltonian dimension must be a power of two >=2.")

    if isspmatrix(hamiltonian):
        sparse_h = hamiltonian.tocsr().astype(COMPLEX_DTYPE)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(sparse_h.data)):
            raise ValueError("Hamiltonian must contain only finite values.")
        hermitian_error = sparse_h - sparse_h.getH()
        residual_h = (
            float(np.max(np.abs(hermitian_error.data)))
            if hermitian_error.nnz
            else 0.0
        )
    else:
        dense_h = np.asarray(hamiltonian, dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(dense_h)):
            raise ValueError("Hamiltonian must contain only finite values.")
        residual_h = float(np.linalg.norm(dense_h - dense_h.conj().T, ord=np.inf))
        sparse_h = csr_matrix(dense_h)
    if residual_h > max(1e-12, tolerance * 10.0):
        raise ValueError(f"Hamiltonian is not Hermitian: residual={residual_h:.3e}.")

    if dimension <= dense_dimension_cutoff:
        values, vectors = eigh(sparse_h.toarray(), check_finite=True)
        energy = float(values[0])
        excited = float(values[1])
        state = vectors[:, 0]
        solver = "scipy.linalg.eigh"
    else:
        v0 = np.linspace(1.0, 2.0, dimension, dtype=np.float64)
        v0 /= np.linalg.norm(v0)
        values, vectors = eigsh(
            sparse_h,
            k=2,
            which="SA",
            tol=tolerance,
            v0=v0.astype(COMPLEX_DTYPE),
            maxiter=max(1000, 50 * dimension),
        )
        order = np.argsort(values)
        energy = float(values[order[0]])
        excited = float(values[order[1]])
        state = vectors[:, order[0]]
        solver = "scipy.sparse.linalg.eigsh"
    gap = excited - energy
    degeneracy_tolerance = max(1e-10, 100.0 * tolerance)
    if gap <= degeneracy_tolerance:
        raise ValueError(
            "Ground state is degenerate or numerically unresolved; a unique target "
            "requires an explicit symmetry-sector/tie-break convention."
        )
    state = phase_fix_state(state)
    residual = float(np.linalg.norm(sparse_h @ state - energy * state))
    if residual > max(1e-10, 100.0 * tolerance):
        raise ArithmeticError(f"Ground-state eigen residual is too large: {residual:.3e}.")
    return GroundStateResult(
        state=state,
        energy=energy,
        first_excited_energy=excited,
        gap=gap,
        residual=residual,
        solver=solver,
    )


def bars_and_stripes_bitstrings(rows: int = 2, columns: int = 6) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    """Enumerate unique row-major Bars-and-Stripes bitstrings."""

    rows = validate_integer(rows, name="rows", minimum=1)
    columns = validate_integer(columns, name="columns", minimum=1)
    patterns: set[str] = set()
    # Bars: each column is constant over rows.
    for column_mask in range(1 << columns):
        row = "".join(str((column_mask >> (columns - 1 - c)) & 1) for c in range(columns))
        patterns.add(row * rows)
    # Stripes: each row is constant over columns.
    for row_mask in range(1 << rows):
        bits = [str((row_mask >> (rows - 1 - r)) & 1) for r in range(rows)]
        patterns.add("".join(bit * columns for bit in bits))
    return tuple(sorted(patterns))


def bars_and_stripes_state(rows: int = 2, columns: int = 6) -> ComplexArray:
    """Return the uniform quantum superposition over unique BAS images."""

    patterns = bars_and_stripes_bitstrings(rows, columns)
    num_qubits = rows * columns
    state = np.zeros(1 << num_qubits, dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE)
    amplitude = 1.0 / math.sqrt(len(patterns))
    for pattern in patterns:
        state[int(pattern, 2)] = amplitude
    return state


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class RandomRealMPSResult:
    """Real-normal raw tensors and their normalized Ran-canonical target."""

    seed: int
    max_bond: int
    bond_dimensions: tuple[int, ...]
    raw_tensors: tuple[ComplexArray, ...]
    canonical_mps: MPS
    state: ComplexArray


def random_real_mps(
    num_qubits: int,
    *,
    max_bond: int,
    seed: int,
) -> RandomRealMPSResult:
    """Generate the paper's real N(0,1) random OBC MPS target."""

    num_qubits = validate_integer(num_qubits, name="num_qubits", minimum=2)
    max_bond = validate_integer(max_bond, name="max_bond", minimum=1)
    seed = validate_integer(seed, name="seed", minimum=0)
    bonds = open_boundary_bond_dimensions(num_qubits, max_bond)
    dimensions = (1, *bonds, 1)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    raw_tensors = tuple(
        np.asarray(
            rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=(dimensions[site], 2, dimensions[site + 1])),
            dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE,
        )
        for site in range(num_qubits)
    )
    raw_mps = MPS(raw_tensors)
    normalized = normalize_mps(raw_mps)
    canonical = canonicalize_to_ran(normalized, normalize=True)
    state = phase_fix_state(mps_to_dense(canonical))
    # Put the canonical MPS in the same phase gauge as the exported target.
    canonical_tensors = [tensor.copy() for tensor in canonical.tensors]
    overlap = np.vdot(mps_to_dense(canonical), state)
    canonical_tensors[0] *= overlap / abs(overlap)
    canonical = MPS(canonical_tensors)
    return RandomRealMPSResult(
        seed=seed,
        max_bond=max_bond,
        bond_dimensions=bonds,
        raw_tensors=raw_tensors,
        canonical_mps=canonical,
        state=state,
    )


__all__ = [
    "GroundStateResult",
    "PAULI_I",
    "PAULI_X",
    "PAULI_Y",
    "PAULI_Z",
    "RandomRealMPSResult",
    "SPIN_X",
    "SPIN_Y",
    "SPIN_Z",
    "bars_and_stripes_bitstrings",
    "bars_and_stripes_state",
    "exact_ground_state",
    "grid_edges",
    "heisenberg_grid_hamiltonian",
    "random_real_mps",
    "single_site_operator",
    "tfim_hamiltonian",
    "two_site_product_operator",
    "xxz_hamiltonian",
]



========================================================================
文件路径: src/rudolph2023/analytic.py

"""Ran analytic ``D_i`` layer adapted to Rudolph's pure-U(4) ansatz."""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass

import numpy as np
from numpy.typing import ArrayLike, NDArray

from .circuit import StaircaseLayer, apply_adjacent_u4, zero_state
from .completion import complete_unitary_from_columns
from .conventions import COMPLEX_DTYPE, infer_num_qubits, validate_normalized_statevector
from .linalg import unitarity_residual, validate_unitary
from .mps import (
    MPS,
    TruncationResult,
    dense_to_mps,
    mps_to_dense,
    truncate_mps,
    validate_mps,
)

ComplexArray = NDArray[np.complex128]


def _preparation_u4_from_tensor(
    tensor: ComplexArray,
    *,
    atol: float,
) -> ComplexArray:
    chi_left, _, chi_right = tensor.shape
    prescribed = np.zeros((4, chi_left), dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE)
    for left_bond in range(chi_left):
        for physical in range(2):
            for right_bond in range(chi_right):
                prescribed[2 * physical + right_bond, left_bond] = tensor[
                    left_bond, physical, right_bond
                ]
    input_columns = tuple(2 * left_bond for left_bond in range(chi_left))
    return complete_unitary_from_columns(
        prescribed,
        input_columns,
        dimension=4,
        atol=atol,
    )


def _terminal_u2_from_tensor(tensor: ComplexArray, *, atol: float) -> ComplexArray:
    chi_left, _, chi_right = tensor.shape
    if chi_right != 1:
        raise ValueError("Terminal MPS tensor must have right bond dimension one.")
    prescribed = np.zeros((2, chi_left), dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE)
    for left_bond in range(chi_left):
        prescribed[:, left_bond] = tensor[left_bond, :, 0]
    return complete_unitary_from_columns(
        prescribed,
        tuple(range(chi_left)),
        dimension=2,
        atol=atol,
    )


def fold_terminal_u2_into_last_u4(
    two_qubit_gates: tuple[ArrayLike, ...],
    terminal_u2: ArrayLike,
) -> tuple[ComplexArray, ...]:
    """Fold the terminal gate as ``(I2 kron U2) @ U4_last``.

    This turns Ran's ``N-1 U(4) + U(2)`` layer into exactly the ``N-1 U(4)``
    macro-layer counted by Rudolph et al.
    """

    gates = tuple(validate_unitary(gate, dimension=4) for gate in two_qubit_gates)
    if not gates:
        raise ValueError("At least one U(4) is required; Rudolph layers need N>=2.")
    terminal = validate_unitary(terminal_u2, dimension=2)
    folded = list(gates)
    folded[-1] = np.kron(np.eye(2, dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE), terminal) @ folded[-1]
    if unitarity_residual(folded[-1]) > 1e-12:
        raise ArithmeticError("Folding the terminal U(2) lost unitarity.")
    return tuple(np.asarray(gate, dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE) for gate in folded)


def _prepare_unfolded(
    num_qubits: int,
    two_qubit_gates: tuple[ComplexArray, ...],
    terminal_u2: ComplexArray,
) -> ComplexArray:
    state = zero_state(num_qubits)
    for left_site, gate in enumerate(two_qubit_gates):
        state = apply_adjacent_u4(
            state, gate, left_site, num_qubits=num_qubits, validate_matrix=False
        )
    tensor = state.reshape((2,) * num_qubits)
    leading = np.moveaxis(tensor, num_qubits - 1, 0)
    transformed = terminal_u2 @ leading.reshape(2, -1)
    return np.moveaxis(
        transformed.reshape(leading.shape), 0, num_qubits - 1
    ).reshape(-1)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Chi2LayerEncoding:
    """Exact chi<=2 encoding and terminal-fold validation diagnostics."""

    layer: StaircaseLayer
    unfolded_u4: tuple[ComplexArray, ...]
    terminal_u2: ComplexArray
    exact_state_error: float
    fold_replay_error: float
    max_unitarity_residual: float


def chi2_mps_to_layer(
    mps: MPS,
    *,
    canonical_atol: float = 1e-12,
    replay_atol: float = 1e-10,
) -> Chi2LayerEncoding:
    """Map a normalized Ran-canonical chi<=2 MPS to one pure-U(4) layer."""

    if not math.isfinite(canonical_atol) or canonical_atol <= 0.0:
        raise ValueError("canonical_atol must be finite and positive.")
    if not math.isfinite(replay_atol) or replay_atol <= 0.0:
        raise ValueError("replay_atol must be finite and positive.")
    tensors = validate_mps(mps)
    if len(tensors) < 2:
        raise ValueError("Rudolph pure-U(4) layers require at least two qubits.")
    if any(max(tensor.shape[0], tensor.shape[2]) > 2 for tensor in tensors):
        raise ValueError("Single-layer exact encoding requires every bond <=2.")
    gram_infinity_residuals = []
    for tensor in tensors:
        matrix = tensor.reshape(tensor.shape[0], -1)
        gram_error = matrix @ matrix.conj().T - np.eye(
            matrix.shape[0], dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE
        )
        gram_infinity_residuals.append(
            float(np.linalg.norm(gram_error, ord=np.inf))
        )
    max_canonical_error = max(gram_infinity_residuals)
    if max_canonical_error > canonical_atol:
        raise ValueError(
            f"MPS is not Ran canonical; infinity residual={max_canonical_error:.3e}."
        )

    unfolded = tuple(
        _preparation_u4_from_tensor(tensor, atol=canonical_atol)
        for tensor in tensors[:-1]
    )
    terminal = _terminal_u2_from_tensor(tensors[-1], atol=canonical_atol)
    folded = fold_terminal_u2_into_last_u4(unfolded, terminal)
    layer = StaircaseLayer(len(tensors), folded)

    target = mps_to_dense(mps)
    unfolded_state = _prepare_unfolded(len(tensors), unfolded, terminal)
    folded_state = layer.prepare()
    exact_error = float(np.max(np.abs(unfolded_state - target)))
    fold_error = float(np.max(np.abs(folded_state - unfolded_state)))
    if exact_error > replay_atol:
        raise ArithmeticError(f"Chi=2 layer does not reproduce its MPS: {exact_error:.3e}.")
    if fold_error > replay_atol:
        raise ArithmeticError(f"Terminal U(2) fold changed the state: {fold_error:.3e}.")
    max_unitarity = max(unitarity_residual(matrix) for matrix in folded)
    return Chi2LayerEncoding(
        layer=layer,
        unfolded_u4=unfolded,
        terminal_u2=terminal,
        exact_state_error=exact_error,
        fold_replay_error=fold_error,
        max_unitarity_residual=max_unitarity,
    )


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class AnalyticStepResult:
    """One verified (D_i) extraction from an exact dense residual."""

    layer: StaircaseLayer
    compression: TruncationResult
    encoding: Chi2LayerEncoding
    compressed_state: ComplexArray
    next_residual: ComplexArray
    input_norm: float
    output_norm: float
    extracted_overlap: complex
    expected_overlap: complex
    extracted_overlap_error: float
    preparation_error: float


def analytic_step(
    residual_state: ArrayLike,
    *,
    validation_atol: float = 1e-10,
) -> AnalyticStepResult:
    """Extract one Ran (D_i) layer without truncating the residual itself."""

    if not math.isfinite(validation_atol) or validation_atol <= 0.0:
        raise ValueError("validation_atol must be finite and positive.")
    residual = validate_normalized_statevector(residual_state)
    num_qubits = infer_num_qubits(residual)
    if num_qubits < 2:
        raise ValueError("Rudolph pure-U(4) encoding requires N>=2.")
    exact_mps = dense_to_mps(residual, normalize=False)
    compression = truncate_mps(exact_mps, max_bond=2, normalize=True)
    encoding = chi2_mps_to_layer(
        compression.mps,
        canonical_atol=1e-12,
        replay_atol=validation_atol,
    )
    compressed_state = mps_to_dense(compression.mps)
    prepared_state = encoding.layer.prepare()
    preparation_error = float(np.max(np.abs(prepared_state - compressed_state)))
    if preparation_error > validation_atol:
        raise ArithmeticError(
            f"Analytic preparation differs from compressed MPS: {preparation_error:.3e}."
        )

    next_residual = encoding.layer.adjoint_apply(residual)
    input_norm = float(np.linalg.norm(residual))
    output_norm = float(np.linalg.norm(next_residual))
    if abs(input_norm - output_norm) > validation_atol:
        raise ArithmeticError("Analytic disentangler failed norm conservation.")
    extracted = complex(next_residual[0])
    expected = complex(np.vdot(compressed_state, residual))
    overlap_error = float(abs(extracted - expected))
    if overlap_error > validation_atol:
        raise ArithmeticError(
            "Analytic overlap identity <0|L^dagger|psi>=<psi_chi2|psi> failed."
        )
    return AnalyticStepResult(
        layer=encoding.layer,
        compression=compression,
        encoding=encoding,
        compressed_state=compressed_state,
        next_residual=next_residual,
        input_norm=input_norm,
        output_norm=output_norm,
        extracted_overlap=extracted,
        expected_overlap=expected,
        extracted_overlap_error=overlap_error,
        preparation_error=preparation_error,
    )


__all__ = [
    "AnalyticStepResult",
    "Chi2LayerEncoding",
    "analytic_step",
    "chi2_mps_to_layer",
    "fold_terminal_u2_into_last_u4",
]

"""Audited small-matrix linear algebra used by analytic and optimized layers."""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass

import numpy as np
from numpy.typing import ArrayLike, NDArray

from .conventions import COMPLEX_DTYPE, REAL_DTYPE, as_complex128, validate_real

ComplexArray = NDArray[np.complex128]
RealArray = NDArray[np.float64]


def unitarity_residual(matrix: ArrayLike) -> float:
    """Return ``||U^dagger U-I||_inf`` or infinity for nonsquare input."""

    array = np.asarray(matrix, dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE)
    if array.ndim != 2 or array.shape[0] != array.shape[1]:
        return math.inf
    identity = np.eye(array.shape[0], dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE)
    return float(np.linalg.norm(array.conj().T @ array - identity, ord=np.inf))


def validate_unitary(
    matrix: ArrayLike,
    *,
    dimension: int | None = None,
    atol: float = 1e-12,
) -> ComplexArray:
    """Return an owned read-only complex128 unitary after strict validation."""

    atol = validate_real(atol, name="atol", minimum=0.0)
    result = as_complex128(matrix, copy=True)
    if result.ndim != 2 or result.shape[0] != result.shape[1]:
        raise ValueError(f"Unitary must be square, got shape {result.shape}.")
    if dimension is not None and result.shape != (dimension, dimension):
        raise ValueError(f"Expected {dimension}x{dimension}, got {result.shape}.")
    residual = unitarity_residual(result)
    if residual > atol:
        raise ValueError(f"Matrix is not unitary; residual={residual:.3e}.")
    result.setflags(write=False)
    return result


def _phase_fix_svd_pair(
    u_matrix: ComplexArray,
    vh_matrix: ComplexArray,
    *,
    pivot_on: str,
) -> tuple[ComplexArray, ComplexArray]:
    fixed_u = np.array(u_matrix, dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE, copy=True)
    fixed_vh = np.array(vh_matrix, dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE, copy=True)
    for index in range(min(fixed_u.shape[1], fixed_vh.shape[0])):
        source = fixed_u[:, index] if pivot_on == "u" else fixed_vh[index, :]
        pivot = int(np.argmax(np.abs(source)))
        magnitude = abs(source[pivot])
        if magnitude == 0.0:
            continue
        phase = source[pivot] / magnitude
        if pivot_on == "u":
            fixed_u[:, index] *= np.conj(phase)
            fixed_vh[index, :] *= phase
        else:
            fixed_vh[index, :] *= np.conj(phase)
            fixed_u[:, index] *= phase
    return fixed_u, fixed_vh


def deterministic_svd(
    matrix: ArrayLike,
    *,
    full_matrices: bool = False,
    pivot_on: str = "u",
) -> tuple[ComplexArray, RealArray, ComplexArray]:
    """Compute SVD with paired-vector phase fixing.

    Exact degenerate singular subspaces remain a BLAS/LAPACK convention and
    are therefore recorded in environment provenance rather than hidden.
    """

    array = as_complex128(matrix)
    if array.ndim != 2 or min(array.shape, default=0) < 1:
        raise ValueError(f"SVD input must be a nonempty matrix, got {array.shape}.")
    if pivot_on not in {"u", "vh"}:
        raise ValueError("pivot_on must be 'u' or 'vh'.")
    u_matrix, singular_values, vh_matrix = np.linalg.svd(
        array, full_matrices=full_matrices
    )
    u_matrix, vh_matrix = _phase_fix_svd_pair(
        u_matrix, vh_matrix, pivot_on=pivot_on
    )
    return (
        np.asarray(u_matrix, dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE),
        np.asarray(singular_values, dtype=REAL_DTYPE),
        np.asarray(vh_matrix, dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE),
    )


def svd_numerical_rank(
    singular_values: ArrayLike,
    matrix_shape: tuple[int, int],
) -> int:
    """Return a guarded machine numerical rank, keeping at least one vector."""

    values = np.asarray(singular_values, dtype=REAL_DTYPE).reshape(-1)
    if values.size == 0 or np.any(values < 0.0) or not np.all(np.isfinite(values)):
        raise ValueError("singular_values must be finite, nonnegative, and nonempty.")
    if len(matrix_shape) != 2 or min(matrix_shape) < 1:
        raise ValueError("matrix_shape must contain two positive dimensions.")
    tolerance = (
        4.0
        * np.finfo(REAL_DTYPE).eps
        * max(matrix_shape)
        * float(values[0])
    )
    return max(1, int(np.count_nonzero(values > tolerance)))


def _orthogonal_complement(occupied: ComplexArray, *, dimension: int) -> ComplexArray:
    target_count = dimension - occupied.shape[1]
    if target_count == 0:
        return np.empty((dimension, 0), dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE)
    basis: list[ComplexArray] = []
    tolerance = 128.0 * np.finfo(REAL_DTYPE).eps * dimension
    for anchor_index in range(dimension):
        vector = np.zeros(dimension, dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE)
        vector[anchor_index] = 1.0
        for _ in range(2):
            if occupied.shape[1]:
                vector -= occupied @ (occupied.conj().T @ vector)
            for previous in basis:
                vector -= previous * np.vdot(previous, vector)
        norm = float(np.linalg.norm(vector))
        if norm <= tolerance:
            continue
        vector /= norm
        pivot = int(np.argmax(np.abs(vector)))
        vector *= np.exp(-1j * np.angle(vector[pivot]))
        basis.append(vector)
        if len(basis) == target_count:
            break
    if len(basis) != target_count:
        raise ArithmeticError("Could not complete the polar null space deterministically.")
    return np.column_stack(basis)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PolarResult:
    """Diagnostics of ``argmax_U Re Tr(E^dagger U)``."""

    unitary: ComplexArray
    singular_values: RealArray
    nuclear_norm: float
    achieved_overlap: complex
    numerical_rank: int
    optimization_rank: int
    unitarity_residual: float


def polar_optimal_unitary(
    environment: ArrayLike,
    *,
    rank_rtol: float | None = None,
) -> PolarResult:
    """Return a deterministic unitary polar factor, including rank-deficient cases.

    ``rank_rtol`` controls the reported diagnostic rank only.  The optimizer
    must retain every machine-resolved nonzero singular direction; otherwise
    a user threshold could silently turn the result into a non-optimal polar
    factor.
    """

    matrix = as_complex128(environment, copy=True)
    if matrix.ndim != 2 or matrix.shape[0] != matrix.shape[1]:
        raise ValueError(f"Environment must be square, got {matrix.shape}.")
    if matrix.shape[0] != 4:
        raise ValueError("Rudolph optimization environments must be 4x4.")
    if rank_rtol is not None:
        rank_rtol = validate_real(rank_rtol, name="rank_rtol", minimum=0.0)

    magnitudes = np.abs(matrix)
    pivot = int(np.argmax(magnitudes))
    scale = float(magnitudes.flat[pivot])
    if scale == 0.0:
        phase = 1.0 + 0.0j
        canonical = matrix
    else:
        phase = complex(matrix.flat[pivot] / scale)
        canonical = matrix / (scale * phase)

    u_matrix, canonical_s, vh_matrix = np.linalg.svd(canonical, full_matrices=True)
    singular_values = np.asarray(canonical_s * scale, dtype=REAL_DTYPE)
    largest = float(singular_values[0]) if singular_values.size else 0.0
    machine_threshold = np.finfo(REAL_DTYPE).eps * matrix.shape[0] * largest
    optimization_rank = int(np.count_nonzero(singular_values > machine_threshold))
    diagnostic_threshold = (
        machine_threshold if rank_rtol is None else rank_rtol * largest
    )
    rank = int(np.count_nonzero(singular_values > diagnostic_threshold))
    right_vectors = vh_matrix.conj().T
    left_support = np.asarray(
        u_matrix[:, :optimization_rank], dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE
    )
    right_support = np.asarray(
        right_vectors[:, :optimization_rank], dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE
    )
    support_factor = left_support @ right_support.conj().T
    left_null = _orthogonal_complement(left_support, dimension=matrix.shape[0])
    right_null = _orthogonal_complement(right_support, dimension=matrix.shape[0])
    unitary = np.asarray(
        phase * (support_factor + left_null @ right_null.conj().T),
        dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE,
    )

    residual = unitarity_residual(unitary)
    nuclear_norm = float(np.sum(singular_values, dtype=REAL_DTYPE))
    achieved = complex(np.vdot(matrix, unitary))
    objective_scale = max(1.0, nuclear_norm)
    if abs(achieved.imag) > 1e-12 * objective_scale:
        raise ArithmeticError("Polar optimum has a non-negligible imaginary objective.")
    if abs(achieved.real - nuclear_norm) > 1e-12 * objective_scale:
        raise ArithmeticError("Polar objective does not equal the environment nuclear norm.")
    if residual > 1e-12:
        raise ArithmeticError(f"Polar factor lost unitarity: {residual:.3e}.")
    return PolarResult(
        unitary=unitary,
        singular_values=singular_values,
        nuclear_norm=nuclear_norm,
        achieved_overlap=achieved,
        numerical_rank=rank,
        optimization_rank=optimization_rank,
        unitarity_residual=residual,
    )


def fractional_unitary_power(
    unitary: ArrayLike,
    exponent: float,
    *,
    schur_atol: float = 1e-11,
) -> ComplexArray:
    """Return the principal-branch fractional power of a unitary.

    A complex Schur functional calculus is used.  Eigenphase ``-1`` is assigned
    principal phase ``+pi``.  The branch convention is part of provenance
    because the paper does not specify it.
    """

    exponent = validate_real(exponent, name="exponent", minimum=0.0, maximum=1.0)
    schur_atol = validate_real(schur_atol, name="schur_atol", minimum=0.0)
    matrix = validate_unitary(unitary)
    dimension = matrix.shape[0]
    if exponent == 0.0:
        return np.eye(dimension, dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE)
    if exponent == 1.0:
        return np.array(matrix, copy=True)

    # Imported lazily so the exact analytic D_i path remains usable in a
    # NumPy-only diagnostic environment.
    from scipy.linalg import schur

    triangular, vectors = schur(matrix, output="complex")
    diagonal = np.diag(triangular)
    off_diagonal = triangular - np.diag(diagonal)
    if np.linalg.norm(off_diagonal, ord=np.inf) > schur_atol:
        raise ArithmeticError("Unitary Schur form is unexpectedly non-diagonal.")
    eigenvalues = diagonal / np.abs(diagonal)
    phases = np.angle(eigenvalues)
    # Freeze only points that are exactly on the cut.  An atol-based test would
    # incorrectly map a legitimate phase just below -pi to +pi, creating an
    # O(1) discontinuity in the fractional power.
    on_negative_real_axis = (eigenvalues.real < 0.0) & (eigenvalues.imag == 0.0)
    phases[on_negative_real_axis] = np.pi
    powered = vectors @ np.diag(np.exp(1j * exponent * phases)) @ vectors.conj().T
    residual = unitarity_residual(powered)
    if residual > 1e-11:
        raise ArithmeticError(f"Fractional power lost unitarity: {residual:.3e}.")
    return np.asarray(powered, dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class DampedUpdate:
    """Complete diagnostics for Rudolph equation (6)."""

    unitary: ComplexArray
    relative_unitary: ComplexArray
    fractional_step: ComplexArray
    learning_rate: float
    unitarity_residual: float


def damped_unitary_update(
    current: ArrayLike,
    optimal: ArrayLike,
    *,
    learning_rate: float = 0.6,
) -> DampedUpdate:
    """Apply ``U <- U (U^dagger U_opt)^r`` exactly as paper equation (6)."""

    rate = validate_real(
        learning_rate, name="learning_rate", minimum=0.0, maximum=1.0
    )
    current_u = validate_unitary(current, dimension=4)
    optimal_u = validate_unitary(optimal, dimension=4)
    relative = np.asarray(current_u.conj().T @ optimal_u, dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE)
    step = fractional_unitary_power(relative, rate)
    updated = np.asarray(current_u @ step, dtype=COMPLEX_DTYPE)
    residual = unitarity_residual(updated)
    if residual > 1e-11:
        raise ArithmeticError(f"Damped update lost unitarity: {residual:.3e}.")
    return DampedUpdate(
        unitary=updated,
        relative_unitary=relative,
        fractional_step=step,
        learning_rate=rate,
        unitarity_residual=residual,
    )


__all__ = [
    "DampedUpdate",
    "PolarResult",
    "damped_unitary_update",
    "deterministic_svd",
    "fractional_unitary_power",
    "polar_optimal_unitary",
    "svd_numerical_rank",
    "unitarity_residual",
    "validate_unitary",
]

"""Verified orchestration of the paper's main ``Iter[D_i O_all]`` protocol."""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass

import numpy as np
from numpy.typing import ArrayLike, NDArray

from .analytic import AnalyticStepResult, analytic_step
from .circuit import StaircaseCircuit
from .conventions import (
    infer_num_qubits,
    phase_fix_state,
    validate_integer,
    validate_normalized_statevector,
    validate_real,
)
from .metrics import FidelityMetrics, fidelity_metrics
from .optimizer import OptimizationResult, optimize_all

ComplexArray = NDArray[np.complex128]


def expected_cumulative_gate_updates(
    num_qubits: int,
    depth: int,
    sweeps_per_depth: int,
) -> int:
    """Return ``(N-1) T sum(k,1..K)`` for sequential growth."""

    num_qubits = validate_integer(num_qubits, name="num_qubits", minimum=2)
    depth = validate_integer(depth, name="depth", minimum=0)
    sweeps_per_depth = validate_integer(
        sweeps_per_depth, name="sweeps_per_depth", minimum=0
    )
    return (num_qubits - 1) * sweeps_per_depth * depth * (depth + 1) // 2


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class DepthRecord:
    """One grow-then-optimize iteration and its cross-checked identities."""

    depth: int
    residual_before: ComplexArray
    analytic: AnalyticStepResult
    candidate_circuit: StaircaseCircuit
    candidate_metrics: FidelityMetrics
    candidate_overlap_identity_error: float
    optimization: OptimizationResult
    optimized_circuit: StaircaseCircuit
    optimized_metrics: FidelityMetrics
    residual_after: ComplexArray
    residual_overlap_identity_error: float
    gate_updates_this_depth: int
    cumulative_gate_updates: int


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class IterDiOAllResult:
    """Complete main-protocol result with target gauge and depth trajectory."""

    input_target: ComplexArray
    canonical_target: ComplexArray
    final_circuit: StaircaseCircuit
    final_prepared_state: ComplexArray
    final_metrics: FidelityMetrics
    max_depth: int
    sweeps_per_depth: int
    learning_rate: float
    depth_records: tuple[DepthRecord, ...]

    @property
    def total_gate_updates(self) -> int:
        return sum(record.gate_updates_this_depth for record in self.depth_records)


def run_iter_di_o_all(
    target_state: ArrayLike,
    *,
    max_depth: int,
    sweeps_per_depth: int,
    learning_rate: float = 0.6,
    validation_atol: float = 1e-10,
) -> IterDiOAllResult:
    """Run the main Rudolph protocol with exact dense residuals.

    At every depth the new analytic layer is prepended, all existing gates are
    optimized for a fixed sweep budget, and the residual is recomputed from
    the optimized complete circuit before the next ``D_i`` extraction.
    """

    max_depth = validate_integer(max_depth, name="max_depth", minimum=0)
    sweeps_per_depth = validate_integer(
        sweeps_per_depth, name="sweeps_per_depth", minimum=0
    )
    rate = validate_real(
        learning_rate, name="learning_rate", minimum=0.0, maximum=1.0
    )
    validation_atol = validate_real(
        validation_atol, name="validation_atol", minimum=0.0
    )
    if validation_atol == 0.0:
        raise ValueError("validation_atol must be strictly positive.")
    input_target = validate_normalized_statevector(target_state)
    canonical_target = phase_fix_state(input_target)
    num_qubits = infer_num_qubits(canonical_target)
    if num_qubits < 2:
        raise ValueError("Rudolph pure-U(4) protocol requires N>=2.")

    circuit = StaircaseCircuit.empty(num_qubits)
    records: list[DepthRecord] = []
    cumulative_updates = 0

    for depth in range(1, max_depth + 1):
        # This must be recomputed after O_all changed every existing gate.
        residual_before = circuit.adjoint_apply(canonical_target)
        analytic = analytic_step(residual_before, validation_atol=validation_atol)
        candidate = circuit.prepend_input_layer(analytic.layer)
        candidate_prepared = candidate.prepare()
        candidate_metrics = fidelity_metrics(canonical_target, candidate_prepared)

        # <target|C L|0> = <C^dagger target|L|0>
        expected_candidate_overlap = complex(
            np.vdot(residual_before, analytic.compressed_state)
        )
        candidate_identity_error = float(
            abs(candidate_metrics.overlap - expected_candidate_overlap)
        )
        if candidate_identity_error > validation_atol:
            raise ArithmeticError(
                "Prepending the analytic layer violated the circuit/residual overlap identity."
            )
        if candidate.depth != depth or candidate.num_gates != depth * (num_qubits - 1):
            raise ArithmeticError("Sequential growth produced an invalid gate count.")

        optimization = optimize_all(
            canonical_target,
            candidate,
            sweeps=sweeps_per_depth,
            learning_rate=rate,
            environment_atol=max(1e-12, validation_atol),
        )
        circuit = optimization.optimized_circuit
        prepared = circuit.prepare()
        optimized_metrics = fidelity_metrics(canonical_target, prepared)
        residual_after = circuit.adjoint_apply(canonical_target)
        # residual[0]=<prepared|target>=conj(<target|prepared>)
        residual_identity_error = float(
            abs(np.conj(residual_after[0]) - optimized_metrics.overlap)
        )
        if residual_identity_error > validation_atol:
            raise ArithmeticError("Optimized residual overlap identity failed.")
        if abs(np.linalg.norm(residual_after) - 1.0) > validation_atol:
            raise ArithmeticError("Optimized circuit inverse did not conserve target norm.")

        updates_this_depth = optimization.total_gate_updates
        expected_this_depth = sweeps_per_depth * depth * (num_qubits - 1)
        if updates_this_depth != expected_this_depth:
            raise ArithmeticError(
                f"Update count mismatch: expected {expected_this_depth}, got {updates_this_depth}."
            )
        cumulative_updates += updates_this_depth
        expected_cumulative = expected_cumulative_gate_updates(
            num_qubits, depth, sweeps_per_depth
        )
        if cumulative_updates != expected_cumulative:
            raise ArithmeticError("Cumulative update count identity failed.")

        records.append(
            DepthRecord(
                depth=depth,
                residual_before=residual_before,
                analytic=analytic,
                candidate_circuit=candidate,
                candidate_metrics=candidate_metrics,
                candidate_overlap_identity_error=candidate_identity_error,
                optimization=optimization,
                optimized_circuit=circuit,
                optimized_metrics=optimized_metrics,
                residual_after=residual_after,
                residual_overlap_identity_error=residual_identity_error,
                gate_updates_this_depth=updates_this_depth,
                cumulative_gate_updates=cumulative_updates,
            )
        )

    final_prepared = circuit.prepare()
    final_metrics = fidelity_metrics(canonical_target, final_prepared)
    expected_total = expected_cumulative_gate_updates(
        num_qubits, max_depth, sweeps_per_depth
    )
    if cumulative_updates != expected_total:
        raise ArithmeticError("Final update count identity failed.")
    if not math.isclose(
        float(np.linalg.norm(final_prepared)),
        1.0,
        rel_tol=0.0,
        abs_tol=validation_atol,
    ):
        raise ArithmeticError("Final circuit state is not normalized.")
    return IterDiOAllResult(
        input_target=input_target,
        canonical_target=canonical_target,
        final_circuit=circuit,
        final_prepared_state=final_prepared,
        final_metrics=final_metrics,
        max_depth=max_depth,
        sweeps_per_depth=sweeps_per_depth,
        learning_rate=rate,
        depth_records=tuple(records),
    )


__all__ = [
    "DepthRecord",
    "IterDiOAllResult",
    "expected_cumulative_gate_updates",
    "run_iter_di_o_all",
]



========================================================================
文件路径: src/rudolph2023/export.py
