"""Provenance-complete ordered-U(4) artifact export and integrity checks."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import math
import os
import platform
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import numpy as np
import scipy
from numpy.typing import ArrayLike
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix, isspmatrix

from .circuit import StaircaseCircuit
from .conventions import (
    QUBIT_ORDER,
    TWO_QUBIT_BASIS,
    validate_integer,
    validate_normalized_statevector,
    validate_real,
)
from .metrics import energy_error_per_site, energy_expectation, fidelity_metrics
from .protocol import IterDiOAllResult, expected_cumulative_gate_updates

SCHEMA_VERSION = "rudolph2023-iter-di-o-all-v2"


def semantic_array_sha256(array: ArrayLike) -> str:
    """Hash dtype, shape, and C-order bytes of an array."""

    materialized = np.ascontiguousarray(np.asarray(array))
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    digest.update(str(materialized.dtype).encode("utf-8"))
    digest.update(json.dumps(materialized.shape).encode("utf-8"))
    digest.update(materialized.tobytes(order="C"))
    return digest.hexdigest()


def file_sha256(path: str | Path) -> str:
    """Return SHA-256 of one file without following any manifest convention."""

    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with Path(path).open("rb") as handle:
        for block in iter(lambda: handle.read(1 << 20), b""):
            digest.update(block)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def _complex_json(value: complex) -> dict[str, float]:
    return {"real": float(value.real), "imag": float(value.imag)}


def _source_tree_provenance() -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Fingerprint the exact Python package sources used for this artifact."""

    package_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    file_hashes = {
        path.name: file_sha256(path)
        for path in sorted(package_root.glob("*.py"), key=lambda item: item.name)
    }
    aggregate = hashlib.sha256(
        json.dumps(file_hashes, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()
    return {
        "algorithm": "sha256",
        "package": "rudolph2023",
        "files": file_hashes,
        "aggregate_sha256": aggregate,
    }


def _require_close(name: str, observed: float, expected: float, *, atol: float) -> None:
    if not math.isfinite(observed) or not math.isfinite(expected):
        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be finite.")
    if not math.isclose(observed, expected, rel_tol=0.0, abs_tol=atol):
        raise ValueError(
            f"{name} mismatch: observed={observed:.17g}, expected={expected:.17g}."
        )


def save_reproduction_artifact(
    result: IterDiOAllResult,
    output_directory: str | Path,
    *,
    target_name: str,
    target_metadata: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    hamiltonian: ArrayLike | Any | None = None,
    ground_energy: float | None = None,
) -> Path:
    """Save ordered gates, states, metrics, environment, and checksums."""

    if not isinstance(result, IterDiOAllResult):
        raise TypeError("result must be IterDiOAllResult.")
    if not isinstance(target_name, str) or not target_name.strip():
        raise ValueError("target_name must be nonempty.")
    output = Path(output_directory).resolve()

    if (hamiltonian is None) != (ground_energy is None):
        raise ValueError("hamiltonian and ground_energy must be provided together.")
    gates = result.final_circuit.gate_array()
    arrays_path = output / "result_arrays.npz"
    arrays: dict[str, np.ndarray] = {
        "ordered_u4": gates,
        "target_state": result.canonical_target,
        "prepared_state": result.final_prepared_state,
    }
    energy_evaluation: dict[str, Any] | None = None
    if hamiltonian is not None and ground_energy is not None:
        if not isinstance(ground_energy, int | float | np.floating):
            raise TypeError("ground_energy must be real.")
        ground_energy = float(ground_energy)
        if not math.isfinite(ground_energy):
            raise ValueError("ground_energy must be finite.")
        if isspmatrix(hamiltonian):
            sparse_h = hamiltonian.tocsr().astype(np.complex128)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(sparse_h.data)):
                raise ValueError("Hamiltonian must contain only finite values.")
            arrays.update(
                {
                    "hamiltonian_csr_data": sparse_h.data,
                    "hamiltonian_csr_indices": sparse_h.indices,
                    "hamiltonian_csr_indptr": sparse_h.indptr,
                    "hamiltonian_shape": np.asarray(sparse_h.shape, dtype=np.int64),
                }
            )
            storage = "csr"
        else:
            dense_h = np.asarray(hamiltonian, dtype=np.complex128)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(dense_h)):
                raise ValueError("Hamiltonian must contain only finite values.")
            arrays["hamiltonian_dense"] = dense_h
            storage = "dense"
        prepared_energy = energy_expectation(result.final_prepared_state, hamiltonian)
        error_per_site = energy_error_per_site(
            result.final_prepared_state, hamiltonian, ground_energy
        )
        energy_evaluation = {
            "storage": storage,
            "ground_energy": ground_energy,
            "prepared_energy": prepared_energy,
            "energy_error_per_site": error_per_site,
        }
    output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    np.savez_compressed(arrays_path, **arrays)
    depth_rows = []
    for record in result.depth_records:
        depth_rows.append(
            {
                "depth": record.depth,
                "candidate_amplitude_fidelity": record.candidate_metrics.amplitude_fidelity,
                "optimized_amplitude_fidelity": record.optimized_metrics.amplitude_fidelity,
                "optimized_squared_fidelity": record.optimized_metrics.squared_state_fidelity,
                "paper_infidelity": record.optimized_metrics.paper_amplitude_infidelity,
                "gate_updates_this_depth": record.gate_updates_this_depth,
                "cumulative_gate_updates": record.cumulative_gate_updates,
                "analytic_overlap_identity_error": record.analytic.extracted_overlap_error,
                "candidate_overlap_identity_error": record.candidate_overlap_identity_error,
                "residual_overlap_identity_error": record.residual_overlap_identity_error,
                "max_sweep_environment_error": max(
                    (
                        sweep.max_environment_identity_error
                        for sweep in record.optimization.sweeps
                    ),
                    default=0.0,
                ),
            }
        )

    manifest = {
        "schema_version": SCHEMA_VERSION,
        "protocol": "Iter[D_i O_all]",
        "target_name": target_name,
        "target_metadata": target_metadata or {},
        "num_qubits": result.final_circuit.num_qubits,
        "depth": result.max_depth,
        "sweeps_per_depth": result.sweeps_per_depth,
        "learning_rate": result.learning_rate,
        "gate_layout": {
            "shape": list(gates.shape),
            "order": "physical-execution layer-major; each layer left-site 0..N-2",
            "qubit_order": QUBIT_ORDER,
            "two_qubit_basis": list(TWO_QUBIT_BASIS),
            "terminal_u2_folded": True,
        },
        "final_metrics": {
            "overlap": _complex_json(result.final_metrics.overlap),
            "amplitude_fidelity": result.final_metrics.amplitude_fidelity,
            "paper_amplitude_infidelity": result.final_metrics.paper_amplitude_infidelity,
            "squared_state_fidelity": result.final_metrics.squared_state_fidelity,
            "squared_infidelity": result.final_metrics.squared_infidelity,
        },
        "energy_evaluation": energy_evaluation,
        "total_gate_updates": result.total_gate_updates,
        "depth_results": depth_rows,
        "semantic_sha256": {
            name: semantic_array_sha256(array) for name, array in arrays.items()
        },
        "source_provenance": _source_tree_provenance(),
        "execution": {
            "argv": list(sys.argv),
            "working_directory": str(Path.cwd().resolve()),
        },
        "reproduction_assumptions": {
            "random_mps_seed": "explicit CLI/API seed; paper does not publish sample seeds",
            "unitary_completion": "deterministic computational-anchor double MGS",
            "fractional_power": "principal complex-Schur branch; eigenphase -pi mapped to +pi",
            "heisenberg_grid_site_order": "row-major open boundaries",
        },
        "numerical_environment": {
            "python": sys.version,
            "platform": platform.platform(),
            "numpy": np.__version__,
            "scipy": scipy.__version__,
            "thread_environment": {
                name: os.environ.get(name)
                for name in (
                    "OMP_NUM_THREADS",
                    "MKL_NUM_THREADS",
                    "OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS",
                    "NUMEXPR_NUM_THREADS",
                )
            },
        },
    }
    manifest_path = output / "manifest.json"
    manifest_path.write_text(
        json.dumps(manifest, indent=2, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    checksums = {
        "manifest.json": file_sha256(manifest_path),
        "result_arrays.npz": file_sha256(arrays_path),
    }
    checksum_path = output / "SHA256SUMS.json"
    checksum_path.write_text(
        json.dumps(checksums, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    return manifest_path


def verify_reproduction_artifact(output_directory: str | Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Verify internal integrity, source identity, metrics, layout, and replay."""

    output = Path(output_directory).resolve()
    checksums = json.loads((output / "SHA256SUMS.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    if set(checksums) != {"manifest.json", "result_arrays.npz"}:
        raise ValueError("Checksum index has an unexpected file set.")
    for relative, expected in checksums.items():
        observed = file_sha256(output / relative)
        if observed != expected:
            raise ValueError(f"File checksum mismatch for {relative}.")
    manifest = json.loads((output / "manifest.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    if manifest.get("schema_version") != SCHEMA_VERSION:
        raise ValueError("Unsupported artifact schema.")
    if manifest.get("protocol") != "Iter[D_i O_all]":
        raise ValueError("Artifact protocol identifier is incorrect.")
    with np.load(output / "result_arrays.npz", allow_pickle=False) as archive:
        arrays = {name: archive[name].copy() for name in archive.files}
    if set(arrays) != set(manifest.get("semantic_sha256", {})):
        raise ValueError("Array archive and semantic-hash index have different keys.")
    for name, array in arrays.items():
        expected = manifest["semantic_sha256"][name]
        if semantic_array_sha256(array) != expected:
            raise ValueError(f"Semantic checksum mismatch for {name}.")
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(array)):
            raise ValueError(f"Array {name} contains non-finite values.")

    required = {"ordered_u4", "target_state", "prepared_state"}
    if not required.issubset(arrays):
        raise ValueError("Artifact is missing required gate/state arrays.")
    gates = arrays["ordered_u4"]
    target = validate_normalized_statevector(arrays["target_state"])
    prepared = validate_normalized_statevector(arrays["prepared_state"])
    num_qubits = validate_integer(
        manifest.get("num_qubits"), name="manifest.num_qubits", minimum=2
    )
    depth = validate_integer(manifest.get("depth"), name="manifest.depth", minimum=0)
    sweeps = validate_integer(
        manifest.get("sweeps_per_depth"),
        name="manifest.sweeps_per_depth",
        minimum=0,
    )
    validate_real(
        manifest.get("learning_rate"),
        name="manifest.learning_rate",
        minimum=0.0,
        maximum=1.0,
    )
    expected_shape = (depth, num_qubits - 1, 4, 4)
    if gates.shape != expected_shape:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Gate array shape {gates.shape} does not match manifest {expected_shape}."
        )
    layout = manifest.get("gate_layout", {})
    if layout.get("shape") != list(expected_shape):
        raise ValueError("gate_layout.shape does not match the stored gates.")
    if layout.get("order") != (
        "physical-execution layer-major; each layer left-site 0..N-2"
    ):
        raise ValueError("Unsupported gate execution order in gate layout.")
    if layout.get("qubit_order") != QUBIT_ORDER:
        raise ValueError("Unsupported qubit order in gate layout.")
    if tuple(layout.get("two_qubit_basis", ())) != TWO_QUBIT_BASIS:
        raise ValueError("Unsupported two-qubit basis in gate layout.")
    if layout.get("terminal_u2_folded") is not True:
        raise ValueError("Artifact does not declare the required terminal-U(2) fold.")
    circuit = StaircaseCircuit.from_gate_array(gates)
    replay = circuit.prepare()
    replay_error = float(np.max(np.abs(replay - prepared)))
    if replay_error > 1e-11:
        raise ValueError(f"Ordered-gate replay mismatch: {replay_error:.3e}.")
    metrics = fidelity_metrics(target, replay)
    overlap = metrics.overlap
    expected_overlap = manifest["final_metrics"]["overlap"]
    serialized = complex(expected_overlap["real"], expected_overlap["imag"])
    if not math.isfinite(serialized.real) or not math.isfinite(serialized.imag):
        raise ValueError("Manifest overlap is non-finite.")
    if abs(overlap - serialized) > 1e-11:
        raise ValueError("Manifest overlap does not match replay.")
    for name, observed in (
        ("amplitude_fidelity", metrics.amplitude_fidelity),
        ("paper_amplitude_infidelity", metrics.paper_amplitude_infidelity),
        ("squared_state_fidelity", metrics.squared_state_fidelity),
        ("squared_infidelity", metrics.squared_infidelity),
    ):
        _require_close(
            f"final_metrics.{name}",
            observed,
            float(manifest["final_metrics"][name]),
            atol=1e-12,
        )

    expected_updates = expected_cumulative_gate_updates(num_qubits, depth, sweeps)
    if manifest.get("total_gate_updates") != expected_updates:
        raise ValueError("Manifest total_gate_updates violates the protocol identity.")
    depth_rows = manifest.get("depth_results")
    if not isinstance(depth_rows, list) or len(depth_rows) != depth:
        raise ValueError("depth_results length does not match circuit depth.")
    for row_depth, row in enumerate(depth_rows, start=1):
        expected_this = sweeps * row_depth * (num_qubits - 1)
        expected_cumulative = expected_cumulative_gate_updates(
            num_qubits, row_depth, sweeps
        )
        if (
            row.get("depth") != row_depth
            or row.get("gate_updates_this_depth") != expected_this
            or row.get("cumulative_gate_updates") != expected_cumulative
        ):
            raise ValueError(f"Depth-row update identity failed at depth {row_depth}.")
        for metric_name in (
            "candidate_amplitude_fidelity",
            "optimized_amplitude_fidelity",
            "optimized_squared_fidelity",
            "paper_infidelity",
            "analytic_overlap_identity_error",
            "candidate_overlap_identity_error",
            "residual_overlap_identity_error",
            "max_sweep_environment_error",
        ):
            value = row.get(metric_name)
            if not isinstance(value, int | float) or not math.isfinite(float(value)):
                raise ValueError(f"Depth metric {metric_name} is non-finite or missing.")

    energy_record = manifest.get("energy_evaluation")
    if energy_record is not None:
        storage = energy_record.get("storage")
        if storage == "csr":
            csr_keys = {
                "hamiltonian_csr_data",
                "hamiltonian_csr_indices",
                "hamiltonian_csr_indptr",
                "hamiltonian_shape",
            }
            if not csr_keys.issubset(arrays):
                raise ValueError("CSR Hamiltonian arrays are incomplete.")
            shape_values = tuple(int(value) for value in arrays["hamiltonian_shape"])
            if len(shape_values) != 2:
                raise ValueError("Hamiltonian shape must have two entries.")
            hamiltonian = csr_matrix(
                (
                    arrays["hamiltonian_csr_data"],
                    arrays["hamiltonian_csr_indices"],
                    arrays["hamiltonian_csr_indptr"],
                ),
                shape=shape_values,
            )
        elif storage == "dense":
            if "hamiltonian_dense" not in arrays:
                raise ValueError("Dense Hamiltonian array is missing.")
            hamiltonian = arrays["hamiltonian_dense"]
        else:
            raise ValueError("Unknown Hamiltonian storage mode.")
        ground_energy = float(energy_record["ground_energy"])
        observed_energy = energy_expectation(replay, hamiltonian)
        observed_error = energy_error_per_site(replay, hamiltonian, ground_energy)
        _require_close(
            "energy_evaluation.prepared_energy",
            observed_energy,
            float(energy_record["prepared_energy"]),
            atol=1e-10,
        )
        _require_close(
            "energy_evaluation.energy_error_per_site",
            observed_error,
            float(energy_record["energy_error_per_site"]),
            atol=1e-11,
        )
    elif any(name.startswith("hamiltonian_") for name in arrays):
        raise ValueError("Hamiltonian arrays exist without an energy-evaluation record.")

    source = manifest.get("source_provenance")
    if source != _source_tree_provenance():
        raise ValueError("Current rudolph2023 source tree differs from artifact provenance.")
    return {
        "status": "verified",
        "schema_version": SCHEMA_VERSION,
        "replay_error": replay_error,
        "amplitude_fidelity": metrics.amplitude_fidelity,
        "source_tree_sha256": source["aggregate_sha256"],
        "energy_verified": energy_record is not None,
    }


__all__ = [
    "SCHEMA_VERSION",
    "file_sha256",
    "save_reproduction_artifact",
    "semantic_array_sha256",
    "verify_reproduction_artifact",
]

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pytest

from rudolph2023.analytic import (
    analytic_step,
    chi2_mps_to_layer,
    fold_terminal_u2_into_last_u4,
)
from rudolph2023.circuit import StaircaseLayer, apply_adjacent_u4, zero_state
from rudolph2023.conventions import normalized_statevector, state_distance_up_to_global_phase
from rudolph2023.linalg import unitarity_residual
from rudolph2023.mps import MPS, canonicalize_to_ran, dense_to_mps, mps_to_dense


def random_unitary(dimension: int, seed: int) -> np.ndarray:
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    matrix = rng.normal(size=(dimension, dimension)) + 1j * rng.normal(
        size=(dimension, dimension)
    )
    q_matrix, _ = np.linalg.qr(matrix)
    return q_matrix


def apply_last_u2(state: np.ndarray, unitary: np.ndarray, num_qubits: int) -> np.ndarray:
    tensor = state.reshape((2,) * num_qubits)
    leading = np.moveaxis(tensor, num_qubits - 1, 0)
    output = unitary @ leading.reshape(2, -1)
    return np.moveaxis(output.reshape(leading.shape), 0, num_qubits - 1).reshape(-1)


def test_terminal_u2_fold_is_exact_for_arbitrary_input_not_only_zero_state() -> None:
    num_qubits = 4
    u4s = tuple(random_unitary(4, seed) for seed in (1, 2, 3))
    # Deliberately asymmetric complex U2 catches Kronecker and multiplication reversals.
    u2 = np.array(
        [[1.0, 1.0j], [1.0j, 1.0]], dtype=np.complex128
    ) / np.sqrt(2.0)
    folded = fold_terminal_u2_into_last_u4(u4s, u2)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(4)
    state = normalized_statevector(
        rng.normal(size=16) + 1j * rng.normal(size=16)
    )
    unfolded_state = state
    for left_site, gate in enumerate(u4s):
        unfolded_state = apply_adjacent_u4(
            unfolded_state, gate, left_site, num_qubits=num_qubits
        )
    unfolded_state = apply_last_u2(unfolded_state, u2, num_qubits)
    folded_state = StaircaseLayer(num_qubits, folded).apply(state)
    assert np.max(np.abs(folded_state - unfolded_state)) < 3e-14
    assert max(unitarity_residual(gate) for gate in folded) < 1e-12


@pytest.mark.parametrize("num_qubits", range(2, 8))
def test_chi2_mps_maps_exactly_to_one_pure_u4_layer(num_qubits: int) -> None:
    source_layer = StaircaseLayer(
        num_qubits,
        tuple(random_unitary(4, 1000 + site) for site in range(num_qubits - 1)),
    )
    state = source_layer.prepare()
    mps = canonicalize_to_ran(dense_to_mps(state), normalize=True)
    assert mps.max_bond_dimension <= 2
    encoding = chi2_mps_to_layer(mps)
    assert len(encoding.layer.matrices) == num_qubits - 1
    assert state_distance_up_to_global_phase(state, encoding.layer.prepare()) < 2e-12
    assert encoding.exact_state_error < 2e-12
    assert encoding.fold_replay_error < 2e-12


@pytest.mark.parametrize("num_qubits", range(2, 8))
def test_general_random_row_isometric_mps_maps_to_layer(num_qubits: int) -> None:
    rng = np.random.default_rng(3000 + num_qubits)
    bonds = [1, *(2 for _ in range(num_qubits - 1)), 1]
    tensors = []
    for site in range(num_qubits):
        left, right = bonds[site], bonds[site + 1]
        raw = rng.normal(size=(2 * right, left)) + 1j * rng.normal(
            size=(2 * right, left)
        )
        q_matrix, _ = np.linalg.qr(raw)
        tensors.append(q_matrix.conj().T.reshape(left, 2, right))
    mps = MPS(tensors)
    state = mps_to_dense(mps)
    assert np.linalg.norm(state) == pytest.approx(1.0, abs=2e-13)
    encoding = chi2_mps_to_layer(mps)
    assert np.max(np.abs(encoding.layer.prepare() - state)) < 3e-13


def test_analytic_layer_exact_on_basis_ghz_and_w_boundary_cases() -> None:
    cases = []
    for num_qubits in range(2, 7):
        for index in range(1 << num_qubits):
            state = np.zeros(1 << num_qubits, dtype=np.complex128)
            state[index] = 1.0
            cases.append(state)
    ghz = np.zeros(32, dtype=np.complex128)
    ghz[0] = ghz[-1] = 1 / np.sqrt(2)
    w_state = np.zeros(32, dtype=np.complex128)
    for site in range(5):
        w_state[1 << site] = 1 / np.sqrt(5)
    cases.extend((ghz, w_state))
    for state in cases:
        result = analytic_step(state)
        assert np.max(np.abs(result.layer.prepare() - state)) < 5e-13
        assert result.compression.squared_fidelity == pytest.approx(1.0, abs=1e-12)


def test_analytic_tolerance_arguments_are_strict_and_separate() -> None:
    mps = canonicalize_to_ran(dense_to_mps(zero_state(3)))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="canonical_atol"):
        chi2_mps_to_layer(mps, canonical_atol=0.0)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="replay_atol"):
        chi2_mps_to_layer(mps, replay_atol=0.0)


def test_product_and_bell_states_are_machine_precision_analytic_cases() -> None:
    product = zero_state(4)
    product_step = analytic_step(product)
    assert abs(product_step.extracted_overlap) == pytest.approx(1.0, abs=1e-13)
    assert np.max(np.abs(product_step.layer.prepare() - product)) < 1e-13

    bell = np.zeros(4, dtype=np.complex128)
    bell[0] = bell[3] = 1 / np.sqrt(2)
    bell_step = analytic_step(bell)
    assert abs(bell_step.extracted_overlap) == pytest.approx(1.0, abs=1e-13)
    assert np.max(np.abs(bell_step.layer.prepare() - bell)) < 1e-13


@pytest.mark.parametrize("seed", range(12))
def test_analytic_step_high_bond_invariants_under_stress(seed: int) -> None:
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    residual = normalized_statevector(
        rng.normal(size=64) + 1j * rng.normal(size=64)
    )
    result = analytic_step(residual)
    assert result.compression.mps.max_bond_dimension <= 2
    assert result.input_norm == pytest.approx(1.0, abs=2e-13)
    assert result.output_norm == pytest.approx(1.0, abs=2e-13)
    assert result.extracted_overlap_error < 2e-12
    assert result.preparation_error < 2e-12
    direct = result.layer.adjoint_apply(residual)
    assert np.max(np.abs(direct - result.next_residual)) < 1e-14
    assert direct[0] == pytest.approx(np.vdot(result.compressed_state, residual), abs=2e-12)


def test_analytic_step_rejects_non_normalized_input() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        analytic_step(np.ones(4, dtype=np.complex128))



========================================================================
文件路径: tests/conftest.py
========================================================================
"""Session-level function-call audit.

The mathematical tests provide independent oracles.  This profiler adds a
separate engineering invariant: every source-defined module function and
method must actually execute in the complete suite, so an untested helper
cannot hide behind a passing top-level smoke test.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import ast
import hashlib
import json
import os
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

SOURCE_ROOT = (Path(__file__).parents[1] / "src" / "rudolph2023").resolve()
SOURCE_PREFIX = os.path.normcase(str(SOURCE_ROOT))
CALLED: set[str] = set()
RUN_STARTED_UTC = ""


def _profile(frame, event: str, _arg):  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    if event != "call":
        return
    filename = os.path.normcase(frame.f_code.co_filename)
    if not filename.startswith(SOURCE_PREFIX):
        return
    try:
        path = Path(filename).resolve()
        relative = path.relative_to(SOURCE_ROOT)
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        return
    if relative.suffix != ".py":
        return
    module = ".".join(relative.with_suffix("").parts)
    function_name = frame.f_code.co_name
    if "self" in frame.f_locals:
        function_name = f"{type(frame.f_locals['self']).__name__}.{function_name}"
    elif "cls" in frame.f_locals and isinstance(frame.f_locals["cls"], type):
        function_name = f"{frame.f_locals['cls'].__name__}.{function_name}"
    CALLED.add(f"{module}:{function_name}")


def _expected_functions() -> set[str]:
    expected: set[str] = set()
    for path in SOURCE_ROOT.glob("*.py"):
        if path.name == "__main__.py":
            continue
        module = path.stem
        tree = ast.parse(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), filename=str(path))
        for node in tree.body:
            if isinstance(node, ast.FunctionDef | ast.AsyncFunctionDef):
                expected.add(f"{module}:{node.name}")
            elif isinstance(node, ast.ClassDef):
                for child in node.body:
                    if isinstance(child, ast.FunctionDef | ast.AsyncFunctionDef):
                        expected.add(f"{module}:{node.name}.{child.name}")
    return expected


def _tree_sha256(root: Path, pattern: str) -> str:
    records: dict[str, str] = {}
    for path in sorted(root.glob(pattern)):
        digest = hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
        records[path.relative_to(root).as_posix()] = digest
    return hashlib.sha256(
        json.dumps(records, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()


def pytest_sessionstart(session) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    del session
    global RUN_STARTED_UTC
    CALLED.clear()
    RUN_STARTED_UTC = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
    sys.setprofile(_profile)


def pytest_runtest_setup(item) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    # The paper-scale regression repeats already-audited functions thousands
    # of times.  Profiling it adds minutes without increasing entry coverage.
    if item.get_closest_marker("slow") is not None:
        sys.setprofile(None)


def pytest_runtest_teardown(item, nextitem) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    del nextitem
    if item.get_closest_marker("slow") is not None:
        sys.setprofile(_profile)


def pytest_sessionfinish(session, exitstatus: int) -> None:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
    del exitstatus
    sys.setprofile(None)
    expected = _expected_functions()
    missing = sorted(expected - CALLED)
    project_root = Path(__file__).parents[1]
    requested = [
        str(value).replace("\\", "/").rstrip("/")
        for value in session.config.getoption("file_or_dir")
    ]
    full_suite = requested in ([], ["tests"])
    record = {
        "schema_version": "rudolph2023-function-call-audit-v2",
        "started_utc": RUN_STARTED_UTC,
        "finished_utc": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "process_id": os.getpid(),
        "pytest_argv": list(session.config.invocation_params.args),
        "full_suite_requested": full_suite,
        "source_tree_sha256": _tree_sha256(SOURCE_ROOT, "*.py"),
        "test_tree_sha256": _tree_sha256(project_root / "tests", "*.py"),
        "expected_count": len(expected),
        "called_count": len(expected & CALLED),
        "missing": missing,
        "status": "passed" if not missing and session.exitstatus == 0 else "failed",
        "scope_note": (
            "Entry-call coverage only; mathematical correctness is established by "
            "independent oracle, property, regression, and cross-implementation tests. "
            "Slow paper-scale tests are not profiled because their source functions "
            "are already exercised by focused tests."
        ),
    }
    audit_root = project_root / "artifacts" / "function_call_audits"
    audit_root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    run_id = RUN_STARTED_UTC.replace(":", "").replace("+", "_").replace(".", "_")
    run_output = audit_root / f"audit_{run_id}_{os.getpid()}.json"
    serialized = json.dumps(record, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n"
    run_output.write_text(serialized, encoding="utf-8")
    if full_suite:
        canonical = project_root / "artifacts" / "function_call_audit.json"
        canonical.write_text(serialized, encoding="utf-8")
    if full_suite and missing and session.exitstatus == 0:
        print("\nFunction-call audit missing source functions:")
        for name in missing:
            print(f"  - {name}")
        session.exitstatus = 1

文件路径: tests/test_completion_circuit.py

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pytest

from rudolph2023.circuit import (
    StaircaseCircuit,
    StaircaseLayer,
    apply_adjacent_u4,
    apply_flat_gate_sequence,
    embed_adjacent_u4,
    zero_state,
)
from rudolph2023.completion import complete_unitary_from_columns
from rudolph2023.conventions import normalized_statevector
from rudolph2023.linalg import unitarity_residual


def random_unitary(seed: int) -> np.ndarray:
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    matrix = rng.normal(size=(4, 4)) + 1j * rng.normal(size=(4, 4))
    q_matrix, _ = np.linalg.qr(matrix)
    return q_matrix


def test_deterministic_completion_preserves_columns_and_is_unitary() -> None:
    prescribed = np.array(
        [[1 / np.sqrt(2), 0], [0, 1], [1j / np.sqrt(2), 0], [0, 0]],
        dtype=np.complex128,
    )
    first = complete_unitary_from_columns(prescribed, (0, 2))
    second = complete_unitary_from_columns(prescribed, (0, 2))
    assert np.array_equal(first, second)
    assert np.array_equal(first[:, (0, 2)], prescribed)
    assert unitarity_residual(first) < 1e-12
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        complete_unitary_from_columns(np.ones((4, 2)), (0, 2))


def test_completion_strict_integer_validation_and_empty_completion() -> None:
    prescribed = np.eye(4, dtype=np.complex128)[:, :1]
    for bad_dimension in (4.0, True):
        with pytest.raises(TypeError):
            complete_unitary_from_columns(
                prescribed, (0,), dimension=bad_dimension  # type: ignore[arg-type]
            )
    for bad_index in (0.9, True):
        with pytest.raises(TypeError):
            complete_unitary_from_columns(
                prescribed, (bad_index,)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
            )
    empty = complete_unitary_from_columns(
        np.empty((3, 0), dtype=np.complex128), (), dimension=3
    )
    assert np.array_equal(empty, np.eye(3))


@pytest.mark.parametrize("num_qubits", [2, 3, 4, 5])
def test_adjacent_executor_matches_full_kronecker_oracle(num_qubits: int) -> None:
    state = normalized_statevector(
        np.arange(1, (1 << num_qubits) + 1) + 1j * np.arange(1 << num_qubits)
    )
    for left_site in range(num_qubits - 1):
        gate = random_unitary(10 + left_site)
        observed = apply_adjacent_u4(state, gate, left_site, num_qubits=num_qubits)
        expected = embed_adjacent_u4(gate, left_site, num_qubits) @ state
        assert np.max(np.abs(observed - expected)) < 2e-14


def test_layer_forward_adjoint_and_full_matrix_order() -> None:
    gates = tuple(random_unitary(seed) for seed in (1, 2, 3))
    layer = StaircaseLayer(4, gates)
    state = normalized_statevector(np.arange(1, 17) + 0.3j * np.arange(16))
    full = np.eye(16, dtype=np.complex128)
    for left_site, gate in enumerate(gates):
        full = embed_adjacent_u4(gate, left_site, 4) @ full
    assert np.max(np.abs(layer.apply(state) - full @ state)) < 3e-14
    assert np.max(np.abs(layer.adjoint_apply(layer.apply(state)) - state)) < 4e-14
    assert np.linalg.norm(layer.prepare()) == pytest.approx(1.0, abs=2e-14)


def test_circuit_prepend_gate_array_and_independent_flat_replay() -> None:
    old = StaircaseLayer(3, (random_unitary(1), random_unitary(2)))
    new = StaircaseLayer(3, (random_unitary(3), random_unitary(4)))
    circuit = StaircaseCircuit.empty(3).prepend_input_layer(old)
    grown = circuit.prepend_input_layer(new)
    state = zero_state(3)
    expected = old.apply(new.apply(state))
    assert np.max(np.abs(grown.prepare() - expected)) < 2e-14
    replay = apply_flat_gate_sequence(state, grown.flat_gates, num_qubits=3)
    assert np.max(np.abs(replay - expected)) < 2e-14
    assert grown.num_gates == 4
    assert grown.gate_array().shape == (2, 2, 4, 4)
    loaded = StaircaseCircuit.from_gate_array(grown.gate_array())
    assert np.max(np.abs(loaded.prepare() - expected)) < 2e-14
    assert np.max(np.abs(grown.adjoint_apply(grown.apply(state)) - state)) < 5e-14


def test_circuit_layout_validation() -> None:
    identity = StaircaseLayer.identity(3)
    assert np.array_equal(identity.prepare(), zero_state(3))
    changed = identity.with_gate(0, random_unitary(99))
    assert np.max(np.abs(changed.matrices[0] - identity.matrices[0])) > 1e-3
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        StaircaseLayer(4, (np.eye(4),))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        StaircaseCircuit.from_gate_array(np.zeros((2, 3, 3, 3)))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        StaircaseCircuit.empty(3).with_flat_matrices([np.eye(4)])
    assert StaircaseCircuit.empty(3).overlap(zero_state(3)) == pytest.approx(1.0)
    assert np.array_equal(
        apply_flat_gate_sequence(zero_state(3), (), num_qubits=3), zero_state(3)
    )
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="inconsistent"):
        apply_flat_gate_sequence(zero_state(2), (), num_qubits=3)

文件路径: tests/test_paper_scale_regression.py

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pytest

from rudolph2023.protocol import run_iter_di_o_all
from rudolph2023.targets import (
    bars_and_stripes_state,
    exact_ground_state,
    heisenberg_grid_hamiltonian,
    random_real_mps,
)


def paper_heisenberg_target() -> np.ndarray:
    ground = exact_ground_state(
        heisenberg_grid_hamiltonian(rows=3, columns=4), tolerance=1e-11
    )
    # Independent high-precision regression value for the 17-edge OBC S=sigma/2 model.
    assert ground.energy == pytest.approx(-6.691680193514956, abs=2e-10)
    assert ground.residual < 2e-10
    return ground.state


@pytest.mark.slow
@pytest.mark.regression
@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("name", "expected_final_amplitude", "target_factory"),
    [
        (
            "heisenberg_3x4",
            0.971655798233501,
            paper_heisenberg_target,
        ),
        ("bas_2x6", 0.996314482709296, lambda: bars_and_stripes_state(2, 6)),
        (
            "random_mps_seed2023",
            0.8724798693348269,
            lambda: random_real_mps(12, max_bond=64, seed=2023).state,
        ),
    ],
)
def test_figure4_main_method_n12_k8_t10_regression(
    name: str,
    expected_final_amplitude: float,
    target_factory,
) -> None:
    target = target_factory()
    result = run_iter_di_o_all(target, max_depth=8, sweeps_per_depth=10)
    assert result.total_gate_updates == 3960, name
    assert result.final_circuit.gate_array().shape == (8, 11, 4, 4)
    assert result.final_metrics.amplitude_fidelity == pytest.approx(
        expected_final_amplitude,
        abs=2e-3,  # allows degenerate-SVD/BLAS gauge differences across platforms
    )
    amplitudes = [record.optimized_metrics.amplitude_fidelity for record in result.depth_records]
    # This is a fixed-target regression observation, not a general theorem for r=0.6.
    assert np.all(np.diff(amplitudes) >= -1e-10)
    assert max(
        sweep.max_environment_identity_error
        for depth in result.depth_records
        for sweep in depth.optimization.sweeps
    ) < 1e-10
    assert max(
        depth.residual_overlap_identity_error for depth in result.depth_records
    ) < 1e-10



========================================================================
文件路径: tests/test_conventions_linalg.py
========================================================================
from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pytest

from rudolph2023.conventions import (
    as_complex128,
    infer_num_qubits,
    normalized_statevector,
    phase_aligned_state,
    phase_fix_state,
    state_distance_up_to_global_phase,
    validate_integer,
    validate_normalized_statevector,
    validate_real,
)
from rudolph2023.linalg import (
    damped_unitary_update,
    deterministic_svd,
    fractional_unitary_power,
    polar_optimal_unitary,
    svd_numerical_rank,
    unitarity_residual,
    validate_unitary,
)


def random_unitary(dimension: int, seed: int) -> np.ndarray:
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    matrix = rng.normal(size=(dimension, dimension)) + 1j * rng.normal(
        size=(dimension, dimension)
    )
    q_matrix, r_matrix = np.linalg.qr(matrix)
    phases = np.diag(r_matrix)
    phases = np.where(np.abs(phases) > 0, phases / np.abs(phases), 1.0)
    return q_matrix @ np.diag(np.conj(phases))


def test_scalar_and_state_validation() -> None:
    assert validate_integer(np.int64(3), name="n", minimum=2) == 3
    assert validate_real(np.float64(0.6), name="r", minimum=0, maximum=1) == 0.6
    with pytest.raises(TypeError):
        validate_integer(True, name="n", minimum=0)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        validate_real(np.inf, name="x")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        infer_num_qubits(np.ones(3))
    array = as_complex128([1.0, 1j])
    assert array.dtype == np.complex128
    state = normalized_statevector([1.0, 1.0])
    assert np.linalg.norm(state) == pytest.approx(1.0)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        validate_normalized_statevector([1.0, 1.0])


def test_global_phase_utilities_are_physical_and_deterministic() -> None:
    reference = normalized_statevector([1.0, 2.0j, -0.5, 0.0])
    candidate = np.exp(0.73j) * reference
    assert state_distance_up_to_global_phase(reference, candidate) < 1e-14
    assert np.max(np.abs(phase_aligned_state(reference, candidate) - reference)) < 1e-14
    fixed = phase_fix_state(candidate)
    pivot = int(np.argmax(np.abs(fixed)))
    assert fixed[pivot].imag == 0.0
    assert fixed[pivot].real > 0.0
    assert np.max(np.abs(fixed - phase_fix_state(reference))) < 1e-14


def test_validate_unitary_and_residual() -> None:
    unitary = random_unitary(4, 1)
    assert unitarity_residual(unitary) < 1e-12
    checked = validate_unitary(unitary, dimension=4)
    assert not checked.flags.writeable
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        validate_unitary(np.ones((4, 4)), dimension=4)
    assert np.isinf(unitarity_residual(np.ones((2, 3))))


@pytest.mark.parametrize("shape", [(7, 4), (4, 7), (4, 4)])
def test_deterministic_svd_reconstructs(shape: tuple[int, int]) -> None:
    rng = np.random.default_rng(sum(shape))
    matrix = rng.normal(size=shape) + 1j * rng.normal(size=shape)
    u_matrix, values, vh_matrix = deterministic_svd(matrix, pivot_on="u")
    reconstructed = (u_matrix * values) @ vh_matrix
    assert np.max(np.abs(reconstructed - matrix)) < 2e-14
    for column in range(min(shape)):
        pivot = int(np.argmax(np.abs(u_matrix[:, column])))
        assert abs(u_matrix[pivot, column].imag) < 2e-15


def test_svd_numerical_rank_discards_only_machine_null_space() -> None:
    values = np.array([1.0, 1e-3, 0.0])
    assert svd_numerical_rank(values, (3, 3)) == 2
    assert svd_numerical_rank(np.zeros(3), (3, 3)) == 1
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        svd_numerical_rank([], (0, 0))


@pytest.mark.parametrize("rank", [0, 1, 2, 4])
def test_polar_optimum_handles_rank_deficiency(rank: int) -> None:
    left = random_unitary(4, 10)
    right = random_unitary(4, 11)
    singular = np.zeros(4)
    singular[:rank] = np.linspace(2.0, 0.5, rank) if rank else []
    environment = left @ np.diag(singular) @ right.conj().T
    result = polar_optimal_unitary(environment)
    assert unitarity_residual(result.unitary) < 1e-12
    assert result.nuclear_norm == pytest.approx(float(np.sum(singular)), abs=1e-12)
    assert result.achieved_overlap.real == pytest.approx(result.nuclear_norm, abs=1e-12)
    assert abs(result.achieved_overlap.imag) < 1e-12
    assert result.numerical_rank == rank


def test_polar_is_at_least_as_good_as_random_unitaries() -> None:
    rng = np.random.default_rng(22)
    environment = rng.normal(size=(4, 4)) + 1j * rng.normal(size=(4, 4))
    result = polar_optimal_unitary(environment)
    random_objectives = [
        np.vdot(environment, random_unitary(4, seed)).real for seed in range(30)
    ]
    assert result.achieved_overlap.real >= max(random_objectives) - 1e-12


def test_polar_rank_rtol_is_diagnostic_and_never_discards_objective() -> None:
    left = random_unitary(4, 901)
    right = random_unitary(4, 902)
    singular = np.array([1.0, 1e-3, 1e-4, 0.0])
    environment = left @ np.diag(singular) @ right.conj().T
    result = polar_optimal_unitary(environment, rank_rtol=1e-2)
    assert result.numerical_rank == 1
    assert result.optimization_rank == 3
    assert result.achieved_overlap.real == pytest.approx(np.sum(singular), abs=2e-12)
    assert unitarity_residual(result.unitary) < 1e-12


def test_fractional_unitary_power_endpoints_diagonal_and_minus_one_branch() -> None:
    phases = np.array([0.2, -0.7, np.pi, 1.4])
    unitary = np.diag(np.exp(1j * phases))
    assert np.array_equal(fractional_unitary_power(unitary, 0.0), np.eye(4))
    assert np.array_equal(fractional_unitary_power(unitary, 1.0), unitary)
    square_root = fractional_unitary_power(unitary, 0.5)
    expected = np.diag(np.exp(0.5j * phases))
    assert np.max(np.abs(square_root - expected)) < 1e-13
    minus_identity_root = fractional_unitary_power(-np.eye(4), 0.5)
    assert np.max(np.abs(minus_identity_root - 1j * np.eye(4))) < 1e-13
    assert unitarity_residual(square_root) < 1e-12


def test_fractional_power_respects_both_sides_of_negative_axis_cut() -> None:
    epsilon = 5e-15
    phases = np.array([-np.pi + epsilon, np.pi - epsilon, -0.4, 0.7])
    unitary = np.diag(np.exp(1j * phases))
    observed = fractional_unitary_power(unitary, 0.6)
    expected = np.diag(np.exp(0.6j * phases))
    assert np.max(np.abs(observed - expected)) < 5e-13
    assert np.angle(observed[0, 0]) < 0.0
    assert np.angle(observed[1, 1]) > 0.0


@pytest.mark.parametrize("seed", range(10))
def test_fractional_power_random_unitary_matches_independent_eigen_oracle(seed: int) -> None:
    vectors = random_unitary(4, seed)
    phases = np.array([-2.2, -0.5, 0.8, 2.1])
    unitary = vectors @ np.diag(np.exp(1j * phases)) @ vectors.conj().T
    observed = fractional_unitary_power(unitary, 0.6)
    expected = vectors @ np.diag(np.exp(0.6j * phases)) @ vectors.conj().T
    assert np.max(np.abs(observed - expected)) < 5e-13


def test_damped_update_is_exact_equation_6_and_has_correct_endpoints() -> None:
    current = random_unitary(4, 30)
    optimal = random_unitary(4, 31)
    update = damped_unitary_update(current, optimal, learning_rate=0.6)
    expected = current @ fractional_unitary_power(current.conj().T @ optimal, 0.6)
    assert np.max(np.abs(update.unitary - expected)) < 1e-14
    assert np.max(
        np.abs(damped_unitary_update(current, optimal, learning_rate=0.0).unitary - current)
    ) < 1e-14
    assert np.max(
        np.abs(damped_unitary_update(current, optimal, learning_rate=1.0).unitary - optimal)
    ) < 2e-13
    assert update.unitarity_residual < 1e-11

文件路径: tests/test_qiskit_adapter.py

from __future__ import annotations

import importlib
import sys
from types import ModuleType

import numpy as np
import pytest

import rudolph2023.qiskit_adapter as adapter
from rudolph2023.circuit import (
    StaircaseCircuit,
    StaircaseLayer,
    embed_adjacent_u4,
)
from rudolph2023.qiskit_adapter import (
    NATIVE_BASIS,
    compile_staircase_native,
    diagnose_qiskit_compilation,
    project_site_to_qiskit,
    project_sites_to_qiskit_qargs,
    qiskit_statevector_q0_msb,
    statevector_fidelity,
    to_qiskit_logical,
)


def _haar(dimension: int, rng: np.random.Generator) -> np.ndarray:
    matrix = rng.normal(size=(dimension, dimension)) + 1j * rng.normal(
        size=(dimension, dimension)
    )
    q_matrix, r_matrix = np.linalg.qr(matrix)
    diagonal = np.diag(r_matrix)
    phases = np.where(np.abs(diagonal) == 0.0, 1.0, diagonal / np.abs(diagonal))
    return np.asarray(q_matrix @ np.diag(phases.conj()), dtype=np.complex128)


def _random_circuit(num_qubits: int, depth: int, *, seed: int) -> StaircaseCircuit:
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    layers = tuple(
        StaircaseLayer(
            num_qubits,
            tuple(_haar(4, rng) for _ in range(num_qubits - 1)),
        )
        for _ in range(depth)
    )
    return StaircaseCircuit(num_qubits, layers)


def test_adapter_import_is_lazy_and_unavailable_error_is_actionable(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    before = "qiskit" in sys.modules
    importlib.reload(adapter)
    assert ("qiskit" in sys.modules) is before

    assert isinstance(adapter.qiskit_available(), bool)
    monkeypatch.setattr(adapter.importlib.util, "find_spec", lambda _name: None)
    assert not adapter.qiskit_available()

    def unavailable(_name: str) -> ModuleType:
        raise ImportError("intentionally unavailable")

    monkeypatch.setattr(adapter.importlib, "import_module", unavailable)
    with pytest.raises(adapter.QiskitUnavailableError, match="optional noise dependencies"):
        adapter.require_qiskit()


def test_site_mapping_preserves_ordered_local_u4_basis() -> None:
    assert tuple(project_site_to_qiskit(site, num_qubits=4) for site in range(4)) == (
        3,
        2,
        1,
        0,
    )
    assert project_sites_to_qiskit_qargs((0, 1), num_qubits=4) == (2, 3)
    assert project_sites_to_qiskit_qargs((1, 2), num_qubits=4) == (1, 2)
    assert project_sites_to_qiskit_qargs((2, 3), num_qubits=4) == (0, 1)


def test_mapping_and_statevector_fidelity_reject_ambiguous_inputs() -> None:
    for width, error in ((True, TypeError), (1, ValueError), (3.0, TypeError)):
        with pytest.raises(error):
            project_site_to_qiskit(0, num_qubits=width)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    for site in (True, 1.5):
        with pytest.raises(TypeError):
            project_site_to_qiskit(site, num_qubits=3)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    for site in (-1, 3):
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="outside"):
            project_site_to_qiskit(site, num_qubits=3)
    with pytest.raises(TypeError, match="tuple"):
        project_sites_to_qiskit_qargs([0, 1], num_qubits=3)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="exactly two"):
        project_sites_to_qiskit_qargs((0,), num_qubits=3)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="ordered adjacent"):
        project_sites_to_qiskit_qargs((1, 0), num_qubits=3)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="ordered adjacent"):
        project_sites_to_qiskit_qargs((0, 2), num_qubits=3)

    reference = np.array([1.0, 2.0j], dtype=np.complex128)
    assert statevector_fidelity(reference, -3.5j * reference) == pytest.approx(
        1.0, abs=2e-15
    )
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="same shape"):
        statevector_fidelity([1.0], [1.0, 0.0])
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="empty"):
        statevector_fidelity([], [])
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="finite"):
        statevector_fidelity([np.nan, 0.0], [1.0, 0.0])
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="nonzero"):
        statevector_fidelity([0.0, 0.0], [1.0, 0.0])


def test_wrong_circuit_types_are_rejected_before_optional_work() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(TypeError, match="StaircaseCircuit"):
        to_qiskit_logical(object())  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    with pytest.raises(TypeError, match="StaircaseCircuit"):
        compile_staircase_native(object())  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    with pytest.raises(TypeError, match="StaircaseCircuit"):
        diagnose_qiskit_compilation(object())  # type: ignore[arg-type]


def test_asymmetric_u4_logical_circuit_matches_full_operator_oracle() -> None:
    pytest.importorskip("qiskit")
    from qiskit.quantum_info import Operator

    circuit = _random_circuit(3, 2, seed=734)
    translated = to_qiskit_logical(circuit)
    observed_state = qiskit_statevector_q0_msb(translated)
    expected_operator = np.eye(1 << circuit.num_qubits, dtype=np.complex128)
    for gate in circuit.flat_gates:
        expected_operator = (
            embed_adjacent_u4(gate.matrix, gate.left_site, circuit.num_qubits)
            @ expected_operator
        )

    np.testing.assert_allclose(observed_state, circuit.prepare(), rtol=0.0, atol=3e-13)
    np.testing.assert_allclose(
        np.asarray(Operator(translated).data),
        expected_operator,
        rtol=0.0,
        atol=3e-13,
    )
    assert translated.count_ops() == {"unitary": circuit.num_gates}


@pytest.mark.parametrize("seed", [4, 19, 907])
def test_multilayer_native_synthesis_matches_dense_executor(seed: int) -> None:
    pytest.importorskip("qiskit")
    circuit = _random_circuit(4, 3, seed=seed)

    native = compile_staircase_native(circuit)
    native_state = qiskit_statevector_q0_msb(native)
    diagnostics = diagnose_qiskit_compilation(circuit, native)

    assert set(native.count_ops()) <= NATIVE_BASIS
    assert "unitary" not in native.count_ops()
    assert "barrier" not in native.count_ops()
    assert statevector_fidelity(circuit.prepare(), native_state) > 1.0 - 3e-13
    assert diagnostics.num_qubits == 4
    assert diagnostics.depth == 3
    assert diagnostics.num_u4_gates == 9
    assert diagnostics.logical_replay_fidelity > 1.0 - 3e-14
    assert diagnostics.native_replay_fidelity > 1.0 - 3e-13
    assert diagnostics.compile_fidelity > 1.0 - 3e-13
    assert diagnostics.logical_phase_aligned_max_abs_error < 3e-13
    assert diagnostics.native_phase_aligned_max_abs_error < 3e-13
    assert diagnostics.native_basis_only


def test_exact_synthesis_preserves_accumulated_global_phase() -> None:
    pytest.importorskip("qiskit")
    first_phase = 0.37
    second_phase = -0.19
    circuit = StaircaseCircuit(
        3,
        (
            StaircaseLayer(
                3,
                (
                    np.exp(1j * first_phase) * np.eye(4, dtype=np.complex128),
                    np.exp(1j * second_phase) * np.eye(4, dtype=np.complex128),
                ),
            ),
        ),
    )
    logical = qiskit_statevector_q0_msb(to_qiskit_logical(circuit))
    native_circuit = compile_staircase_native(circuit)
    native = qiskit_statevector_q0_msb(native_circuit)
    expected_phase = first_phase + second_phase

    assert abs(np.angle(logical[0]) - expected_phase) < 3e-13
    assert abs(np.angle(native[0]) - expected_phase) < 3e-13
    np.testing.assert_allclose(native, logical, rtol=0.0, atol=3e-13)
    assert set(native_circuit.count_ops()) <= NATIVE_BASIS


def test_barriers_can_be_retained_or_removed_without_changing_state() -> None:
    pytest.importorskip("qiskit")
    circuit = _random_circuit(3, 2, seed=81)
    retained = compile_staircase_native(
        circuit,
        insert_barriers=True,
        remove_inserted_barriers=False,
    )
    removed = compile_staircase_native(
        circuit,
        insert_barriers=True,
        remove_inserted_barriers=True,
    )

    assert retained.count_ops()["barrier"] == circuit.num_gates - 1
    assert "barrier" not in removed.count_ops()
    assert diagnose_qiskit_compilation(circuit, retained).native_basis_only
    np.testing.assert_allclose(
        qiskit_statevector_q0_msb(retained),
        qiskit_statevector_q0_msb(removed),
        rtol=0.0,
        atol=3e-13,
    )


def test_qiskit_type_width_and_option_error_branches() -> None:
    pytest.importorskip("qiskit")
    from qiskit import QuantumCircuit

    circuit = _random_circuit(3, 1, seed=12)
    with pytest.raises(TypeError, match="insert_barriers"):
        to_qiskit_logical(circuit, insert_barriers=1)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    with pytest.raises(TypeError, match="insert_barriers"):
        compile_staircase_native(circuit, insert_barriers=1)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    with pytest.raises(TypeError, match="remove_inserted_barriers"):
        compile_staircase_native(
            circuit,
            remove_inserted_barriers=1,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        )
    with pytest.raises(TypeError, match="QuantumCircuit"):
        adapter.remove_barriers(object())
    with pytest.raises(TypeError, match="QuantumCircuit"):
        qiskit_statevector_q0_msb(object())
    with pytest.raises(TypeError, match="native_circuit"):
        diagnose_qiskit_compilation(circuit, object())
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="qubit count"):
        diagnose_qiskit_compilation(circuit, QuantumCircuit(2))


def test_empty_staircase_translates_to_identity_without_spurious_gates() -> None:
    pytest.importorskip("qiskit")
    circuit = StaircaseCircuit.empty(3)
    logical = to_qiskit_logical(circuit, insert_barriers=True)
    native = compile_staircase_native(circuit, insert_barriers=True)
    diagnostics = diagnose_qiskit_compilation(circuit, native)

    assert logical.count_ops() == {}
    assert native.count_ops() == {}
    assert diagnostics.num_u4_gates == 0
    assert diagnostics.compile_fidelity == pytest.approx(1.0, abs=2e-15)
    assert diagnostics.native_basis_only

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pytest

from rudolph2023.conventions import normalized_statevector, phase_aligned_state
from rudolph2023.mps import (
    MPS,
    canonicalize_to_ran,
    dense_to_mps,
    mps_inner_product,
    mps_norm,
    mps_to_dense,
    normalize_mps,
    open_boundary_bond_dimensions,
    ran_isometry_residuals,
    truncate_mps,
    validate_mps,
)


def random_state(num_qubits: int, seed: int) -> np.ndarray:
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    return normalized_statevector(
        rng.normal(size=1 << num_qubits) + 1j * rng.normal(size=1 << num_qubits)
    )


def test_mps_validation_rejects_invalid_boundaries_bonds_and_physical_dimension() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        validate_mps([])
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        MPS([np.ones((2, 2, 1))])
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        MPS([np.ones((1, 3, 1))])
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        MPS([np.ones((1, 2, 2)), np.ones((3, 2, 1))])


def test_mps_norm_pipeline_rejects_finite_values_that_overflow_contraction() -> None:
    huge = MPS([np.array([[[1e308], [1e308]]], dtype=np.complex128)])
    with pytest.raises(FloatingPointError, match="overflowed|non-finite"):
        mps_inner_product(huge, huge)
    with pytest.raises(FloatingPointError, match="overflowed|non-finite"):
        mps_norm(huge)
    with pytest.raises(FloatingPointError, match="overflowed|non-finite"):
        normalize_mps(huge)
    with pytest.raises(FloatingPointError, match="overflowed|non-finite"):
        canonicalize_to_ran(huge)
    with pytest.raises(FloatingPointError, match="overflowed|non-finite"):
        truncate_mps(huge, max_bond=1)


def test_mps_norm_pipeline_rejects_underflowed_zero_norm() -> None:
    tiny = MPS([np.array([[[1e-300], [0.0]]], dtype=np.complex128)])
    assert mps_norm(tiny) == 0.0
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="zero MPS"):
        normalize_mps(tiny)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("num_qubits", range(2, 8))
def test_dense_mps_exact_round_trip(num_qubits: int) -> None:
    state = random_state(num_qubits, 100 + num_qubits)
    mps = dense_to_mps(state)
    assert np.max(np.abs(mps_to_dense(mps) - state)) < 2e-14
    assert mps_norm(mps) == pytest.approx(1.0, abs=2e-14)
    assert mps.norm() == pytest.approx(1.0, abs=2e-14)


def test_mps_inner_product_matches_numpy_vdot() -> None:
    first = random_state(5, 1)
    second = random_state(5, 2)
    first_mps = dense_to_mps(first)
    second_mps = dense_to_mps(second)
    assert len(first_mps) == 5
    assert sum(1 for _ in first_mps) == 5
    assert first_mps[0].shape[0] == 1
    assert first_mps.copy() is not first_mps
    assert mps_inner_product(first_mps, second_mps) == pytest.approx(
        np.vdot(first, second), abs=2e-14
    )


def test_normalize_and_ran_canonicalization_preserve_state() -> None:
    state = 3.7 * random_state(6, 7)
    mps = dense_to_mps(state)
    normalized = normalize_mps(mps)
    canonical = canonicalize_to_ran(normalized)
    assert np.max(np.abs(mps_to_dense(normalized) - state / np.linalg.norm(state))) < 3e-14
    assert np.max(np.abs(mps_to_dense(canonical) - mps_to_dense(normalized))) < 5e-14
    assert max(ran_isometry_residuals(canonical)) < 3e-14


def test_chi2_truncation_has_bond_cap_and_self_consistent_metrics() -> None:
    state = random_state(8, 8)
    source = dense_to_mps(state)
    result = truncate_mps(source, max_bond=2)
    assert result.mps.max_bond_dimension <= 2
    dense = mps_to_dense(result.mps)
    amplitude = abs(np.vdot(state, dense))
    assert amplitude == pytest.approx(result.normalized_overlap, abs=2e-13)
    assert amplitude**2 == pytest.approx(result.squared_fidelity, abs=3e-13)
    assert result.output_norm == pytest.approx(1.0, abs=2e-13)
    assert max(result.isometry_residuals) < 2e-13
    assert all(weight >= 0.0 for weight in result.discarded_weights)
    assert result.total_discarded_weight == pytest.approx(
        sum(result.discarded_weights), abs=0.0
    )


@pytest.mark.parametrize("normalize", [False, True])
def test_truncation_of_unnormalized_input_reports_consistent_norms(
    normalize: bool,
) -> None:
    dense = 3.25 * random_state(6, 808)
    source = dense_to_mps(dense)
    result = truncate_mps(source, max_bond=2, normalize=normalize)
    output = mps_to_dense(result.mps)
    expected_amplitude = abs(np.vdot(dense, output)) / (
        np.linalg.norm(dense) * np.linalg.norm(output)
    )
    assert result.input_norm == pytest.approx(3.25, abs=5e-13)
    assert result.output_norm == pytest.approx(np.linalg.norm(output), abs=5e-13)
    assert result.normalized_overlap == pytest.approx(expected_amplitude, abs=5e-13)
    if normalize:
        assert result.output_norm == pytest.approx(1.0, abs=5e-13)


def test_single_bond_truncation_matches_eckart_young_oracle() -> None:
    state = random_state(4, 19)
    result = truncate_mps(dense_to_mps(state), max_bond=2)
    # At the central 4x4 bipartition the optimal rank-2 normalized state is explicit.
    u_matrix, singular, vh_matrix = np.linalg.svd(state.reshape(4, 4), full_matrices=False)
    rank_two = (u_matrix[:, :2] * singular[:2]) @ vh_matrix[:2]
    rank_two = normalized_statevector(rank_two.reshape(-1))
    # Multi-bond TT-SVD also restricts the edge bonds, which are already <=2.
    observed = phase_aligned_state(rank_two, result.mps.to_dense())
    assert np.linalg.norm(observed - rank_two) < 1e-12


def test_open_boundary_bond_profile() -> None:
    assert open_boundary_bond_dimensions(12, 64) == (
        2,
        4,
        8,
        16,
        32,
        64,
        32,
        16,
        8,
        4,
        2,
    )
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        open_boundary_bond_dimensions(1, 2)

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pytest

from rudolph2023.circuit import (
    StaircaseCircuit,
    StaircaseLayer,
    apply_adjacent_u4,
    zero_state,
)
from rudolph2023.conventions import normalized_statevector
from rudolph2023.linalg import damped_unitary_update, polar_optimal_unitary
from rudolph2023.optimizer import (
    build_suffix_states,
    environment_overlap_identity,
    forward_sweep,
    gate_environment,
    local_state_matrix,
    optimize_all,
)


def random_unitary(seed: int) -> np.ndarray:
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    matrix = rng.normal(size=(4, 4)) + 1j * rng.normal(size=(4, 4))
    q_matrix, _ = np.linalg.qr(matrix)
    return q_matrix


def random_circuit(num_qubits: int, depth: int, seed: int) -> StaircaseCircuit:
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    layers = []
    for _layer in range(depth):
        gates = []
        for _site in range(num_qubits - 1):
            matrix = rng.normal(size=(4, 4)) + 1j * rng.normal(size=(4, 4))
            q_matrix, _ = np.linalg.qr(matrix)
            gates.append(q_matrix)
        layers.append(StaircaseLayer(num_qubits, tuple(gates)))
    return StaircaseCircuit(num_qubits, tuple(layers))


def random_state(num_qubits: int, seed: int) -> np.ndarray:
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    return normalized_statevector(
        rng.normal(size=1 << num_qubits) + 1j * rng.normal(size=1 << num_qubits)
    )


def test_local_state_matrix_matches_manual_axis_permutation() -> None:
    state = np.arange(16, dtype=np.complex128) + 1j * np.arange(16)[::-1]
    observed = local_state_matrix(state, 1, num_qubits=4)
    expected = np.moveaxis(state.reshape(2, 2, 2, 2), (1, 2), (0, 1)).reshape(4, -1)
    assert np.array_equal(observed, expected)


def test_suffix_cache_matches_independent_tail_replay() -> None:
    circuit = random_circuit(4, 2, 10)
    target = random_state(4, 11)
    suffixes = build_suffix_states(target, circuit)
    gates = circuit.flat_gates
    for start in range(len(gates) + 1):
        expected = target.copy()
        for gate in reversed(gates[start:]):
            expected = apply_adjacent_u4(
                expected,
                gate.matrix.conj().T,
                gate.left_site,
                num_qubits=4,
                validate_matrix=False,
            )
        assert np.max(np.abs(suffixes[start] - expected)) < 2e-14


@pytest.mark.parametrize("left_site", [0, 1, 2])
def test_environment_identity_equals_direct_global_overlap(left_site: int) -> None:
    prefix = random_state(4, 20)
    suffix = random_state(4, 21)
    gate = random_unitary(22)
    environment = gate_environment(
        prefix, suffix, left_site=left_site, num_qubits=4
    )
    via_environment, via_state, error = environment_overlap_identity(
        environment,
        gate,
        prefix,
        suffix,
        left_site=left_site,
        num_qubits=4,
    )
    assert error < 2e-14
    assert via_environment == pytest.approx(via_state, abs=2e-14)


def test_environment_every_matrix_element_matches_independent_full_replay() -> None:
    circuit = random_circuit(4, 2, 702)
    target = random_state(4, 703)
    gates = circuit.flat_gates
    gate_index = 3
    prefix = zero_state(4)
    for gate in gates[:gate_index]:
        prefix = apply_adjacent_u4(
            prefix,
            gate.matrix,
            gate.left_site,
            num_qubits=4,
            validate_matrix=False,
        )
    suffix = target.copy()
    for gate in reversed(gates[gate_index + 1 :]):
        suffix = apply_adjacent_u4(
            suffix,
            gate.matrix.conj().T,
            gate.left_site,
            num_qubits=4,
            validate_matrix=False,
        )
    environment = gate_environment(
        prefix,
        suffix,
        left_site=gates[gate_index].left_site,
        num_qubits=4,
    )
    for row in range(4):
        for column in range(4):
            basis = np.zeros((4, 4), dtype=np.complex128)
            basis[row, column] = 1.0
            replay = zero_state(4)
            for index, gate in enumerate(gates):
                matrix = basis if index == gate_index else gate.matrix
                replay = apply_adjacent_u4(
                    replay,
                    matrix,
                    gate.left_site,
                    num_qubits=4,
                    validate_matrix=False,
                )
            direct = np.vdot(target, replay)
            assert np.vdot(environment, basis) == pytest.approx(direct, abs=3e-14)


def naive_forward_sweep(
    target: np.ndarray,
    circuit: StaircaseCircuit,
    rate: float,
) -> StaircaseCircuit:
    old_gates = circuit.flat_gates
    updated: list[np.ndarray] = []
    for index, gate in enumerate(old_gates):
        prefix = zero_state(circuit.num_qubits)
        for previous, matrix in zip(old_gates[:index], updated, strict=True):
            prefix = apply_adjacent_u4(
                prefix,
                matrix,
                previous.left_site,
                num_qubits=circuit.num_qubits,
                validate_matrix=False,
            )
        suffix = target.copy()
        for future in reversed(old_gates[index + 1 :]):
            suffix = apply_adjacent_u4(
                suffix,
                future.matrix.conj().T,
                future.left_site,
                num_qubits=circuit.num_qubits,
                validate_matrix=False,
            )
        environment = gate_environment(
            prefix,
            suffix,
            left_site=gate.left_site,
            num_qubits=circuit.num_qubits,
        )
        optimal = polar_optimal_unitary(environment).unitary
        updated.append(
            damped_unitary_update(gate.matrix, optimal, learning_rate=rate).unitary
        )
    return circuit.with_flat_matrices(updated)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("rate", [0.0, 0.6, 1.0])
def test_cached_forward_sweep_matches_naive_gauss_seidel(rate: float) -> None:
    circuit = random_circuit(4, 2, 30)
    target = random_state(4, 31)
    cached, record = forward_sweep(target, circuit, learning_rate=rate)
    naive = naive_forward_sweep(target, circuit, rate)
    assert np.max(np.abs(cached.gate_array() - naive.gate_array())) < 5e-12
    assert np.max(np.abs(cached.prepare() - naive.prepare())) < 5e-12
    assert record.max_environment_identity_error < 5e-14
    assert record.replay_error < 5e-14
    assert len(record.gate_updates) == circuit.num_gates
    assert record.amplitude_gain == pytest.approx(
        record.amplitude_fidelity_after - record.amplitude_fidelity_before
    )
    assert record.squared_fidelity_gain == pytest.approx(
        record.squared_fidelity_after - record.squared_fidelity_before
    )


def test_learning_rate_zero_is_byte_stable_and_rate_one_reaches_each_local_polar() -> None:
    circuit = random_circuit(3, 1, 40)
    target = random_state(3, 41)
    unchanged, _ = forward_sweep(target, circuit, learning_rate=0.0)
    assert np.max(np.abs(unchanged.gate_array() - circuit.gate_array())) < 2e-14

    full, record = forward_sweep(target, circuit, learning_rate=1.0)
    assert full.num_gates == circuit.num_gates
    for update in record.gate_updates:
        assert update.amplitude_after == pytest.approx(
            update.environment_nuclear_norm, abs=2e-12
        )
        assert update.amplitude_after >= update.amplitude_before - 2e-12
    for previous, following in zip(
        record.gate_updates[:-1], record.gate_updates[1:], strict=True
    ):
        assert previous.overlap_after == pytest.approx(
            following.overlap_before, abs=3e-13
        )


def test_optimize_all_uses_fixed_budget_and_exact_update_count() -> None:
    circuit = random_circuit(5, 3, 50)
    target = random_state(5, 51)
    result = optimize_all(target, circuit, sweeps=4, learning_rate=0.6)
    assert len(result.sweeps) == 4
    assert result.total_gate_updates == 4 * 3 * 4
    assert result.requested_sweeps == 4
    assert result.optimized_circuit.depth == 3
    assert np.linalg.norm(result.optimized_circuit.prepare()) == pytest.approx(1.0, abs=1e-12)


def test_damped_updates_record_negative_gains_without_false_monotonic_assertion() -> None:
    # This fixed case proves principal-branch r=.6 is not a general monotone
    # line search; the implementation must record the loss rather than silently
    # replace Eq. (6) with a different phase-aligned update.
    seed = 127
    circuit = random_circuit(3, 1, 100 + seed)
    target = random_state(3, 500 + seed)
    _updated, record = forward_sweep(target, circuit, learning_rate=0.6)
    assert min(update.squared_fidelity_gain for update in record.gate_updates) < -0.1

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import sys
from dataclasses import replace
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace

import numpy as np
import pytest

import rudolph2023.cli as cli
import rudolph2023.native_noise_protocol as protocol
from rudolph2023.export import file_sha256, save_reproduction_artifact

    EncodingNoiseEvaluation,
    LoadedReproductionArtifact,
    evaluate_circuit_under_washington,
    load_reproduction_artifact,
    run_artifact_native_noise_protocol,
)
from rudolph2023.protocol import run_iter_di_o_all
from rudolph2023.targets import exact_ground_state, random_real_mps, tfim_hamiltonian

    CompactWashingtonDevice,
